- R20, R76: device A attaches a file to a new task → on device B the task appears with an attachment marker while the content is not there yet; in the card the attachment is shown as pending, the sync icon names one pending attachment → the content arrives without any user action, the row shows the size. The storage refuses the file → the task still arrives, there is no sync error, the attachment waits. A tap on a pending attachment that is already in the storage → the file is downloaded and opened; one that is not there yet → the row says the download failed.
- R140: the per-file limit is 10 bytes → attach an 11-byte file → adding returns a limit error, the task has no attachment, the content is not in the attachments folder. The per-task limit is 10 bytes, the task already has a 6-byte file → attach a second 6-byte one → an error; delete the first → the same second file is added. A 16 MiB file with a 64 MiB limit → adding succeeds, the size and SHA-256 match, the content on disk is the same; a second device downloads it through the folder. Adding to a task that does not exist → an error, no content in the attachments folder.
- R141: an attachment of 10 bytes → `GET /api/file` with `Range: bytes=2-4` → `206`, `Content-Range: bytes 2-4/10`, the body is bytes 2–4; `bytes=8-` and `bytes=-3` → the matching parts; `bytes=20-` and `bytes=5-3` → `416` with `Content-Range: bytes */10`; `bytes=abc` → `200` with the whole file; `HEAD` → headers without a body. A file shown inline keeps its type and `Content-Security-Policy` in a partial response.
- R142: open the data folder → `stat` of the folder and of `blobs/` is `0700`, of the database and of an attachment file `0600`; after a line is written to the log `log.txt` is also `0600`. Create a backup → `backups/` is `0700`, the backup file `0600`. Restore a backup with an attachment into a clean folder → the attachment file is `0600`. A folder created by an earlier version with other permissions gets them on opening; `backups/` with permissions `0755` and a backup with `0644` → after opening `0700` and `0600`.

- R143: Android, open task "a" with the note "x", put the cursor in the note and type nothing → on another device the note becomes "y", sync → tap elsewhere → the note shows "y", and after sync the other device shows "y" too. With the helper running, start Lists → the helper has released the shortcut; quit Lists → the helper has taken it again.
- R116: macOS, a new installation → the icon is in the menu bar, the icon is in the Dock. Turn both on, close and reopen the app → no icon in the Dock, an icon in the menu bar. - R117: macOS, tasks "a", "b", "c", "d", "e" in "Today" → click "b", ⌘-click "d" → "b" and "d" are selected; ⌘-click "b" → "d" is selected. Select "b", "c", "d", in the context menu of "c" choose "Priority → High" → all three have high priority; ⌘Z → the priorities are as before. The same three, Space → all three are completed; ⌘Z → all three are open. The same three, ⌫ → all three are in the Trash; ⌘Z → all three are back. Select "a" and "b", delete, open "Trash", ⌘A, context menu → a single item "Restore" → both tasks are back. With "b" and "c" selected, right-click "e" → the menu of the single task "e".
- R124: macOS, record the "Show Lists" shortcut in the settings, close the main window, switch to another app, press the shortcut → Lists is active, the main window is in front. Press it again → Lists is hidden. Minimise the main window to the Dock, switch to another app, press the shortcut → the window is restored and in front. Quit Lists with "Quick entry without opening Lists" on, press the shortcut → nothing happens, the quick-entry shortcut works.

The same three onto "Renovation" → the project holds "r", "a", "b", "c". With "a" and "b" selected, drag "d" → only "d" moves, and "d" is selected. In "Home", task "e" with a subtask and task "f" are selected, drop onto "Renovation" → "f" is moved, "e" stays in "Home". - R129: task "a" in "Inbox", there is a list "Home" and a project "Renovation" in "Home" with task "r" → "Move to list" → below the lists, a group "Projects" with "Renovation"; choose "Renovation" → the project holds "r", "a", and "a" is in the list "Home"; undo → "a" is first in "Inbox". For "a" in the project choose "Home" → "a" is in "Home" at the top level, the project holds only "r". For task "c" with a subtask the group "Projects" is unavailable; for "r", "Renovation" is unavailable.
- R129: in "Inbox" select "a", "b" and "c" with a subtask, "Move to list" → "Renovation" → "a" and "b" are moved, "c" stays in "Inbox"; one undo brings back "a" and "b".
- R129: the card of a new task in "Inbox", choose "Renovation" in the bar, title "buy tiles", subtask "glue" → save → the project holds "r", "buy tiles", and the latter has the subtask "glue". The line "buy glue @Renovation" in the quick-entry window with the list "Inbox" → the task is in "Inbox", it did not go to "Renovation".
- R118: the web interface, tasks "a" (low priority), "b", "c" in "Today" → "Select" → the header shows "Selected: 0", the actions at the bottom are unavailable, there is no field for a new task. Tap "a" and "b" → "Selected: 2", no card opens. "More" → "Priority" → "High" → the mode is closed, a banner "Changed: 2"; "Undo" → "a" has low priority again, "b" has none. "Select", "a" and "b", "Delete" → a banner "Deleted: 2". Open "Trash", "Select", "a" and "b" → a single "Restore" button at the bottom; press it → the Trash is empty, a banner "Restored: 2"; "Undo" → both are in the Trash again.
- R134: the web server with a password → four sign-in requests with a wrong password at once → each gets 401, and the page `/` answers without waiting for them. Eight connections from another client start a sign-in with `Content-Length: 2000` and send no body → `/` and a sign-in with the right password answer at once. Twenty wrong passwords at once → five 401 answers and fifteen 429. A sign-in with `Content-Length` over 4 KB → 413. Five wrong passwords in a row → the sixth attempt, even with the right password, gets 429 with `Retry-After`; from another address in `X-Forwarded-For` with `LISTS_TRUST_PROXY=1` the sign-in goes through, without this variable it also gets 429.
- R134: OpenID Connect, start a sign-in 1001 times → completing the first sign-in is rejected as unknown, completing the second goes through. `LISTS_WEB_URL=https://…` → the session cookie has `Secure`; without it or with `http://` it does not.
- R135: the web server, two zones: +14 and −12 hours. Quick entry "east 9:00" from the eastern zone and "west 9:00" from the western one → the first task has the later date. "Today" in the eastern zone shows both tasks, in the western one only "west". A request without the zone header, or with an empty or invalid value, answers 200 by the zone of the server.
- R145: macOS and Android, WebDAV without encryption → "Encrypt the storage…" → different passphrases → confirming is unavailable; the same ones → after encryption the field holds the passphrase, the button has changed to "Change passphrase…"; the second device shows an error about the passphrase, and syncs after it is entered and saved; "Change passphrase…" on the first device → after a restart the second asks for the new one.
- R144: the web server with a password → `GET /healthz` without a cookie answers 200 and `ok`, while `/api/overview` without a cookie answers 401. `lists-web healthcheck` with the address of a running server exits with 0, with an address nobody listens on, with 1.
- R146: the web server, it is 10:00, the browser is subscribed with the defaults. Task "a" with a reminder at 10:01, "b" due today at 10:02, "c" due tomorrow without a time → at 10:01 one notification "a", at 10:02 one "b", tomorrow at 09:00 one "c"; a repeated check in the same minute sends nothing. Choose "15 minutes before" and "At the due time", a task due at 11:00 → notifications at 10:45 and at 11:00. The summary at 08:00 and two tasks for today → at 08:00 "Tasks due today or overdue: 2". Notifications off → nothing.
- R148: a browser in the zone `Europe/Berlin` subscribed in summer, a reminder on 26 October at 10:00 → a notification at 10:00 Berlin time, that is, at 09:00 UTC. The push service answers 410 → the server holds no subscription. Sign-out → no subscription. The body of the notification decrypts with the keys of the browser; the VAPID signature verifies with the public key of the server. A subscription address `http://…` → 400.
- R149: the page is open and stored, the server is stopped → a reload shows the page and "The server cannot be reached."; "Try again" after the server is started shows the tasks.
- R150: the web server with a password → "API tokens…" → "script", "Read" → the token is shown once; the list shows "script", "Read", the creation time, "never used". `api-tokens.json` holds no token string. Restart the server → the token works. "Revoke" → a request with it gets 401.
- R151: a "Read" token → `GET /api/overview` and `GET /api/tasks?scope=today` answer 200 without a cookie; `POST /api/call` answers 403. A "Read and write" token → `POST /api/call` with `quickAdd` without `X-Lists` creates a task; `undo` with this token undoes it, not an edit made in the browser. Any token → `POST /api/tokens`, `POST /api/logout`, `POST /api/notifications/subscribe` answer 403.
- R152: 600 token requests in a minute answer 200, the 601st answers 429 with `Retry-After`. Five requests with a wrong token → the sixth, and a sign-in with the right password from the same address, get 429.
- R155: on A, a list "Home" sorted by due date and with a colour, an archived list "Archive", tasks with a due date, start date, reminder, priority, tags, note, a completed and an open subtask, "won't do", the repeat "every 2 weeks on Mon and Thu, 5 times", a project with a task, a filter on "Home" with priority at least high → export to JSON → import on an empty B → the export of B matches the export of A except for the identifiers; a second import changes nothing. The export of one list holds only its tasks. A file of version 2 is rejected with a request to update the app.
- R156: "Дом" ("Home") with the task "Купить краску" ("Buy paint") (due 2026-10-07 18:30, high priority, tag "дом" ("home"), a note of two lines, the completed subtask "кисть" ("brush")) and the task "Обои" ("Wallpaper"), closed as "won't do" → `- [ ] Купить краску — due 2026-10-07 18:30 · high priority · #дом`, under it the lines of the note and `  - [x] кисть`, then `- [x] ~~Обои~~ — won't do`.
- R94: open a task with a due date, high priority and a tag → the title line has no background, no line under it and no chevron; on the left three chips, on the right the icons of start date, repeat, reminder, "#", subtask and attachment. Click the repeat icon and choose "Every week" → the repeat chip has moved to the left, there is no icon for it on the right. Tab from the note goes through the chips, then through the icons, then into the title. Esc closes the card.
- R83: open a task, type "buy" in the note and, without leaving the note, quit the app (macOS, ⌘Q) or press Home and wait for the app to be unloaded (Android) → open the app → the note holds "buy". The same with the title of the task.
- R64: Android, press "+", go to the note of the compact card and type three lines → all three are visible, the row of fields is below them. Type seven more → the note field is five lines high, the tenth line with the cursor is visible, and the title, the row of fields and the bar with the "Save" button are visible above the keyboard. Expand the card and collapse it back → all ten lines are in place, the cursor is in the note. The same in the quick-entry window.
- R64: Android, press "+", type "Позвонить в банк завтра 10:00 !!" ("Call the bank tomorrow 10:00 !!") → in the row of fields, on the left the chips "Tomorrow, 10:00" and "Medium", on the right the icons of start date, repeat and file, with no icons for due date and priority; in the bar, the list on the left, "Save" on the right. Press "Save" → the task is created, due tomorrow at 10:00 with medium priority, the card is empty and open, "Save" is unavailable.
- R102: a new installation → level "Errors", no log file; sync with an unreachable storage → `log.txt` has an ERROR line with the source sync; level "Events", a successful sync pass → an INFO line with the numbers received and sent; level "Off", a sync failure → the file does not change; close and reopen the app → the level is the same; the log is over 1 MB, a new record → the earlier content is in `log.1.txt`, the record in `log.txt`; "Clear" → neither file exists. A task "Секрет" ("Secret") with a note, the storage password "pw-123", level "Detailed", a sync pass with a failure and one without → the log holds neither "Секрет" nor "pw-123". The storage answers 409 to an attachment upload → one ERROR line with the source attachments, `upload of blobs/<aa>/<8 characters>…` and `HTTP 409`, without the full hash, the file name or the task title; the same pass again and after a restart → still one ERROR line; an hour later → a second one, with "failing since"; the attachment has uploaded → an INFO line "went through after failing since"; the storage answers 500 to a download → an ERROR line at once; the content in the storage does not match the hash → an ERROR line; the content is missing from the storage → an INFO line, after 59 minutes no ERROR, after an hour an ERROR with "waiting since"; a download on request failed → an ERROR line; add an attachment from a file that does not exist, save a push address that is not http(s) → ERROR lines. Android: complete from the widget a task that does not exist → an ERROR line with the source widget; pass the app a file that cannot be read → an ERROR line with the source attachments, without the file name and path; deny the notification permission → an ERROR line with the source notify; revoke the permission for exact alarms → one ERROR line with the source notify, the following recalculations do not repeat it, grant the permission back → an INFO line. macOS: the keychain did not save the password → an ERROR line with the source sync and the error code, without the password.
- R101: macOS, open the settings → five tabs at the top: "General", "Tasks", "Notifications", "Sync", "Backups"; "General" holds appearance, text, sidebar and completed tasks, and no sync. On "Sync" choose WebDAV and type an address, go to "General" and back → the address is in place. Android, open the settings → a full-screen list of five sections and "Import…"; tap "Notifications" → the title shows "Notifications" and an arrow, the screen shows only the notification settings; "Back" → the list of sections; "Back" → the settings are closed. The "Sync" section has "Save and sync", the others do not.
- R100: Android, settings → "Text size" → "Largest" → the text of the settings and of the list behind them is larger at once; close and reopen the app → the size is the same; open the quick-entry window from "Share" → its text is the same size; choose "Default" → the size is as before. A system font scale of 1.3 and the step "Default" → the text is larger than with a system scale of 1.0.
- R99: Android, open a task → the card is as high as its content, the list is visible above it; drag the handle up → the card fills the screen, the handle is below the status bar, the title, the note and the row of fields are the same; drag the handle down → the card is again as high as its content and open; drag the handle down once more → the card is closed. Expand the card, close it, open another task → the card is as high as its content.
- R98: macOS, the main window is open, press ⌘H, press the global shortcut from another app → only the quick-entry window is on screen, no main window. Press Esc → no quick-entry window, Lists is hidden. Click the Lists icon in the Dock → the main window is in place. Press ⌘H, click the Lists icon in the menu bar → only its window is on screen, no main window. Click in another app → no icon window, Lists is hidden; click the Lists icon in the Dock → the main window is in place. Press ⌘H, click the icon in the menu bar, choose "Open Lists" → the main window is in place, no icon window.
- R97: Android, press "+", type "Draft", close the card with the "Back" button → a bar "Draft discarded" with "Restore" at the bottom; press "Restore" → the card is open, the title holds "Draft". Close it again and wait 5 seconds → no bar; press "+" → the card is empty. Type text, close and press "+" at once → the card is empty, no bar. Close an empty card → no bar. Press "+", type text, drag the handle up → the card is expanded, the text is in place; drag the handle down → the card is compact and open; drag the handle down once more → the card is closed, the bar is shown.
- R95: Android, open "Today" with an overdue task → the top bar has no title, above the tasks a large "Today" with a yellow star, the headings "Overdue" and "Today" are capsules (R159), the marks are grey for tasks of any list, the "New task" button is blue. Open a list → the icon of the list in its colour before the large title. Open the panel of lists and long-press a list → its settings are open; the rows have no settings icon.
- R105: Android, open the list "Work" → the bar has a "More" button after the sync icon; press it and "Configure" → the settings of the list "Work" are open. Open "Inbox" → "Configure" opens the settings of "Inbox"; open a filter → the settings of the filter. Open "Today" or a tag → no "More" button. Open search in a list → no "More" button.
- R107: Android, place the small widget and choose "Today"; there are task "a" due yesterday and task "b" due today → the heading "Today", the rows "a" and "b". Tap the mark of "a" → the widget is left with "b", in the app "a" is completed. Tap "b" → the app opens in "Today". Place the large widget and choose the list "Work" → the heading "Work", the open tasks of the list in its order; tap the heading → the app opens in the list "Work". More tasks than fit → the last row "N more". No tasks → "No tasks".
- R108: Android, a widget with the list "Work", tap "+" → the quick-entry window is open; create "c" in it → task "c" is in the default list of quick entry.
- R109: Android, tasks "a", "b", "c", "d" in a list → long-press "b", "Select" → the bar shows "Selected: 1", "b" has a filled circle, there is no new task button. Tap "d" → "Selected: 2"; tap "d" again → "Selected: 1". A drag over the circles from "a" to "c" → "Selected: 3"; without letting go, back to "b" → "a" and "b" are selected. "Delete" → the mode is closed, "a" and "b" are in the Trash, a banner "Deleted: 2"; "Undo" → both are in place. Select "a" and "c", "Due" → "Tomorrow" → both are due tomorrow, a banner "Changed: 2"; "Undo" → neither has a due date. Select "a" and "c", "Complete" → both are completed, "Completed: 2"; "Undo" → both are open. Select "a", "Back" → the mode is closed, nothing has changed. Select "a" and a task already in the list "Work", "Move to list" → "Work" → "Moved: 1". Delete "a" and "b", open "Trash", long-press "a" → the menu has "Select" and "Restore"; "Select", tap "b" → the bar has a single "Restore" button; tap it → the Trash is empty, a banner "Restored: 2"; "Undo" → both tasks are in the Trash again.
- R96: Android, open a task with a due date, a priority and a tag, without a start date, repeat or reminder → under the note, on the left the chips of due date, priority and tag, on the right the icons of start date, repeat, reminder, tag and file; there are no "Start" and "Due" chips without a value. Tap the repeat icon and choose "Every week" → a repeat chip has appeared on the left, there is no repeat icon on the right. A task with three subtasks, one of them completed → lines between the subtasks, round marks, the completed one grey with a tick and not struck through.
- R77: Android, a phone, a list is open → turn the device on its side → the window stays in portrait; open the quick-entry window from "Share" over the app in landscape → the quick-entry window is in landscape, its card is expanded (R64).
- R45: the card of a new task with the due date 2026-10-05 and the repeat "every week" → create → the task has the rule "every week"; complete it → the due date is 2026-10-12.
- R48: the note `# План` + a blank line + `**важно** и *срочно*, см. [сайт](https://example.org)` → ranges: a first-level heading on "План", bold on "важно", italic on "срочно", a link with the address `https://example.org` on "сайт"; `# `, `**`, `*`, `[` and `](https://example.org)` are marked as markup. Offsets are counted in UTF-16 units: in the note `😀 **да**` the bold starts at 5.
- R48: the note `Https://example.org/A и [x](HTTPS://EXAMPLE.ORG)` → a link with the address `https://example.org/A` on `Https://example.org/A` and a link with the address `https://EXAMPLE.ORG` on "x"; the text of the note does not change.
- R49: a note of two paragraphs `**один**` and `*два*`, the cursor in the second → in the first the asterisks are hidden, in the second they are visible; the cursor leaves the note → both pairs are hidden.
- R50: any note → after styling, the text of the field is byte for byte equal to what was typed.
- R51: the note `- [ ] хлеб` → a click on the checkbox → in the core the note is `- [x] хлеб`; another click → `- [ ] хлеб`.
- R52: `- хлеб` with the cursor at the end → Return → `- хлеб` and a new line `- `; Return again → the second line is empty, without a marker. `9. девять` → Return → a new line `10. `. `  - [x] сделано` → Return → a new line `  - [ ] `. `- хлеб и молоко` with the cursor before "и" → Return → `- хлеб ` and `- и молоко`.
- R71: the note `см. https://example.org`, the cursor at the end of the note → a click on macOS or a tap on Android on the address → the address `https://example.org` is passed to the system, the cursor stays at the end, the text is the same; a click on "см." → no address is passed, the cursor is in the word.
- R53: the note `<img src=x onerror=alert(1)> [x](javascript:alert(1))` → the page has no `img` element and no link with `javascript:`; `<img src=x onerror=alert(1)> x` is visible.
- R78: the setting is off → "Today" has no block of events, calendar access is not requested. The setting is turned on, access is granted, the calendars hold "Standup" at 09:00, "Meeting with the bank" at 14:30 and the all-day "Anya's birthday" for today → a block of three rows above the tasks, in this order: "Anya's birthday" without a time, "09:00 Standup", "14:30 Meeting with the bank"; the count of "Today" is unchanged. The check on the calendar with "Standup" is cleared → two rows in the block. No tasks for today → the block is in place, "All done for today" below it. "Upcoming" is open → no block.

## What is implemented

State as of 2026-10-05, version 0.1.0.

**Core** — all requirements R1–R23, R26, R28, R29 (test `password_is_kept_out_of_the_database_and_asked_for_again_after_restart`) and the rules of [sync.md](sync.md). Verified by the tests `core/tests/store.rs`, `core/tests/sync.rs`, `core/tests/webdav.rs` and the unit tests in `core/src` (`make test`).

**macOS** — lists and their settings, tasks expanding in place, subtasks as rows with a title (R10), repeats, tags, attachments, search, the Trash, undo of deletion and completion, the quick-entry window on a global shortcut, the menu bar icon, the "Share" extension, an item in the "Services" menu, reminders, sync settings (WebDAV and folder). Verified by running: the app builds and starts, tasks are created by the link `lists://add`, the quick-entry line is parsed, the window with a list and an expanded task looks as intended, sync with Android through a local WebDAV server works both ways, the password is read from the keychain at start, the Russian localisation is picked up from the system language. Not verified by pressing: the global shortcut, the "Share" extension (the system has registered it), the "Services" menu, notifications, drag and drop.

**Android** — the same data and actions: the list screen with the panel of lists, the editor as a bottom sheet, swipes (right to complete, left for the due date, right from the left edge for the panel of lists), a menu on long press, the quick-entry window from "Share", from the menu of selected text, from a shortcut and from a quick settings tile, reminders, background sync every 15 minutes. Verified on an Android 16 emulator: start, quick entry with line parsing, receiving text through "Share", the list, the editor, WebDAV setup and sync with macOS, the interface in English and in Russian, the password in the app settings is stored as ciphertext and sync continues after a restart. Not verified: the tile, the menu of selected text, receiving files, notifications.

**Projects, filters, font** (R30–R35) — the core is verified by tests (`projects_are_tasks_with_a_view_of_their_own`, `saved_filters_*`, sync both ways). On macOS, Android and the web, projects and filters are added to the sidebar and build; checked live are the font setting on macOS and the API of the web server (`projects_and_saved_filters_over_the_api`); the project and filter screens were not checked by pressing. The font size on macOS does not change the counts in the sidebar or the section headings.

**Notifications** (R36) — the core computes the notification plan (`planned_notifications`, test `notifications_follow_reminders_due_dates_and_settings`), the settings exist on macOS and Android. On the Android emulator it was checked that the settings dialog opens and that an alarm is set for a task due at a time; the notification appearing has not been observed on any platform. Choice of sound: on the Android emulator the "Sound" row creates the channel "Reminders" if it does not exist yet and opens its system screen; with the notification permission granted the screen has a sound row, without it the system shows that notifications are blocked and offers to turn them on. On macOS the debug step `sound` checked that the system sounds are offered, that the chosen sound is copied to `Library/Sounds` of the group container, and that an empty or unknown name gives the standard sound; the settings window with the choice was not looked at on screen. Nothing was checked by ear: neither the sound played when chosen nor the sound of an arriving notification, on either platform. That the macOS notification centre finds the copied sound by name follows from the documentation and remains unverified. The web interface has no notifications.

**The system refusing notifications on macOS** (R111, #203) — verified on 2026-10-11 by a debug script in a hidden window, with a separate data folder. On that day the system answered the debug build "allowed": for a task due "tomorrow 10:00" the step `notifications` prints one request, accepted by the system, for 9:45, and that the settings have no row. The refusal by the system could not be reproduced, so the answer was fed to the model by the steps `notifications:denied` and `notifications:allowed`: after the first the row is shown and the log has a record of the refusal, after the second there is no row and the request is with the system. The row with the button is fully visible in the settings snapshot (`settingsshot`); the link the button opens brings up the section "Notifications → Lists" of System Settings on screen. Not verified: a refusal coming from the system itself; a notification appearing on screen. Why the system refused Lists until 2026-10-10 has not been established (#203).

**The reminder at the due time by default** (R36, #206) — what a saved choice means is decided by one function on each platform. On Android it is checked by the tests `NotifyLeadsTest` (`r36_*`): no choice, the previous default before and after the update, other options, "off". On macOS there are no Swift tests: the debug step `leads` prints the answer of the same function for the same cases. The update was verified on 2026-10-11 on the Mac of the author of the project with a debug build: the settings held "15 minutes", after the start they held "at the due time" and a mark that the update had run. Not verified: the update on an Android device and a notification appearing on screen at the due time.

**The day of the due date in a notification** (R36, #280) — 2026-10-15. Before the fix, both platforms wrote the text of the notification at the time of planning, and the day of the due date was counted from that day. Android sets the alarms again only on a data change, a start and a sync; macOS hands the plan to the system again only when the key, the time or the title changes — this is visible in the code and was not reproduced on a Mac. Reproduced on an API 36 emulator without a window: a task due 2026-10-16 without a time, the alarm at the default 09:00 set on 2026-10-15, the emulator clock moved to 2026-10-16 08:59:50 → the notification shade shows "Due: tomorrow" (`dumpsys notification`). After the fix, on Android the alarm carries the due date itself, and the receiver of the alarm builds the text at the time of showing; the same run → "Due: today". The test `ReminderContentTest` (`r36_*`) checks that the alarm carries the due date and not a finished text. On macOS the text is still handed to the system in advance, so the day is counted from the day the notification is planned for, and the due date is now part of the signature of the plan. Verified by the debug step `notifications` in a hidden run with a separate data folder on 2026-10-15: a task due tomorrow → "Срок: сегодня" ("Due: today") at 2026-10-16 09:00, a task due today at 18:00 → "Срок: сегодня, 18:00" ("Due: today, 18:00"). Not verified: a notification appearing on the screen of the Mac and on the phone of the author of the project; on macOS a notification the system showed late on the next day (the Mac was asleep) names the day counted from the planned day.

**Reminders on Android** (R36, R66, #207) — verified on 2026-10-11 on an API 36 emulator without a window; the alarms are taken from `adb shell dumpsys alarm`, the notifications from the event log of the system. A due date at a time set on the device: the alarm is set, the notification arrived; without the "Alarms & reminders" permission, 2 min 52 s after the due time, within the window the system allowed. A due date that arrived by sync through a folder: with the app in the background and with the process ended, the next scheduled sync run brought the tasks and set the alarms; the run came 3.5 minutes later than scheduled. Several reminder options (R36): before the fix, a task with the options "at the due time" and "5 minutes before" kept one alarm, the last, because alarms differed only by task; now there are two, and with the exact alarm permission both notifications arrived to the second, at 11:37:00 and 11:42:00, the second replacing the first in the notification shade. Not verified: the phone of the author of the project, from which the report #207 came; the power saving mode, in which the system postpones scheduled runs; sync through WebDAV and push.

**A subtask with only a title** (R10, R12, R13, R22, R32, R38, R112, S42, C29, I9; #205, #202) — the core is verified by the tests `r10_*`, `r112_data_from_before_the_rule_is_brought_to_it`, `r22_a_subtask_is_found_inside_its_task`, `s42_what_an_earlier_version_sends_is_brought_to_the_rule_of_subtasks`, `c29_what_another_client_gives_a_subtask_is_removed`, `i9_a_subtask_keeps_its_title_and_one_level`, `r32_tasks_closed_as_wont_do_or_in_the_trash_are_tasks_of_the_project`, `r10_the_copy_of_a_project_keeps_the_fields_of_its_tasks`, `s42_a_task_of_a_project_read_before_the_mark_of_its_project_keeps_its_fields` and `r92_a_database_from_before_the_number_was_kept_is_of_the_first_schema`; without bringing the data to the rule the tests R112, S42 and C29 fail, and with bringing it after each batch of sync the test about a task of a project fails. Web: the tests `r10_*` and `r22_*` in `web/tests/api.rs`, the page walked through in a browser without a window. macOS: a debug script with a separate data folder went through adding, editing the title in the row (Return, Esc, the selection leaving), completing, "won't do", deleting and undo, the contents of the right-click menu, a task of a project with a card and a subtask row, search by the title of a subtask; on screen the title is edited in the row and is shown in full (#202). Android: the tests `SubtasksTest` and a run on an API 36 emulator without a window — editing on a tap on the title, "Done" and leaving the field save, an empty title is not saved, a long-press menu of four items, a task of a project with a card, search. Not verified: a double click with the mouse and selecting and copying text in the field on macOS by hand; the "Rename" item of the menu on Android; bringing the real data of the author of the project to the rule — it happens at the first start of the new version, and a backup is made before it.

**Subtasks in the card of a new task** (R113, #199) — core: the test `r113_the_subtasks_of_a_new_task_are_added_in_the_order_they_were_typed`. Android: the tests `r113_*` in `NewTaskEntryTest` and a run on an API 36 emulator without a window: a title, three subtasks, one left in the field → the database has a task with three subtasks in the order typed, "tickets tomorrow !!" without a due date; "Back" creates nothing, "Restore" brings back the title, the rows and the text of the field; the quick-entry window saves subtasks. macOS: a debug script with a separate data folder went through two subtasks, text left in the field, Esc, a title with a date in words, a task in a project and the quick-entry window with real key presses; the rows and the field are visible in a snapshot of the window. Not verified: a mouse click on the icon and on the delete button of a row on macOS; the expanded card, the project view and rotating the screen on Android.

**The list name under the task title and the priority as a bar** (R72, #225) — the decisions of the author of the project of 2026-10-12 are recorded in #225, together with the snapshots of the prototype used to choose: the list name under the task title after the model of Things, without groups by list; the mark centred on the height of the row and smaller; the priority as a bar after the mark, red, orange and blue, with the space for it kept; the list name stays in colour. Android: the colour of the bar is checked by the test `r72_the_bar_*` in `RowOriginTest`; on an API 36 emulator without a window (1080×2400), in "Today", by `uiautomator dump` there is a separate text with the list name under the task title ("Home and family", "Inbox", "Home and family – Kitchen renovation"), tasks with a priority have an element with the description "High", "Medium", "Low", there are no "!" signs; by a screenshot the titles start on one vertical line, the bars come in three colours. macOS: a hidden transparent instance of the debug build with a separate data folder, the steps `origins` and `winshot`: in "Today" the list name is under the task title, the mark is centred, the bars come in three colours; in the view of a single list the row is one line and the bar is shorter; on an open card the bar stands next to the mark. The web interface: the page "Today" drawn into a file by a headless browser from the same data set: the mark centred on the row, bars in three colours, no "!" signs. Not verified by eye on a real screen: the look on a phone and in a visible Mac window, the dark appearance in the real implementation (in the prototype it was captured only on Android), a large text size, the row of the selected task and the selection mode on Android.

**The list name in the row** (R72, #198) — Android: the tests `r72_*` in `RowOriginTest`; on an API 36 emulator without a window, in "Today", a task of the list "Home" has "Home" after its title (a task from "Inbox" had nothing then; after the fix this was not rechecked on the emulator). macOS: the debug step `origins` with a separate data folder prints for "Today", "All" and search: "Home" for a task of a list, "Home – renovation" for a task of a project in a list, "Inbox" for a task of "Inbox" and "Inbox – project" for a task of a project in "Inbox" (fixed after the regression run of 2026-10-11: at first tasks of "Inbox" had no name); in the view of a list and in "Inbox", nothing; on screen the list name is set in its colour after the signs, the project name is dimmed. Web: a change to one function of the page, the script of the page parses (`node --check`). Not verified: the web page in a browser; the colour of the name on Android by eye; legibility of light list colours in small type in the light and dark appearance; truncation of a long name in a narrow row.

**Regression run of 2026-10-11 on the data of the author of the project** (R37, R38, R72, R113) — after remarks by the author of the project: a task of "Inbox" in mixed views shows "Inbox"; Esc in the card of a new task saves it, ⌘Z moves the created task to the Trash; the list scrolls to a card opened at the bottom edge of the window. Verified by a debug build in a hidden window on a copy of the data of the author of the project with sync off: in "Today" five tasks show "Inbox", the others "Site" and "Recurring" in the colours of the lists, in "Inbox" there is no label; a double click on a subtask starts editing the title in the row; by real key presses — Esc in the subtask field and in the title creates the task with the typed subtasks, ⌘Z removes it, Esc without a title creates nothing, Return in an empty subtask field does nothing; the card of a task from the bottom of "Inbox" is fully visible in a snapshot of the window; a task due in two minutes is handed to the system for the due time, and the system showed the notification as a banner (log `usernoted`). Not verified: scrolling to the card in a visible window; the "Cancel" button of the quick-entry window; Android after the fix of the "Inbox" label.

**Leaving the quick-entry window on macOS** (R114) — the decision of the author of the project of 2026-10-11: the quick-entry window has its own requirements, Esc in it discards what was typed, ⌘Return saves. Verified by real key presses in a hidden instance of the debug build with a separate data folder: a title typed in the quick-entry window, Esc → the window is closed, no task; a title typed, ⌘Return → the task is created; in the card of a list Esc still creates the task (R37). Not verified: the "Cancel" button by a click, the menu bar window, Esc while the date picker is open in the quick-entry window.

**One permanent notification on Android** (R104, R106, #200) — the tests `r104_the_heading_*`, `r104_without_overdue_tasks_*`, `r104_nothing_for_today_*` in `TodayNoticeTest` and `r106_the_update_*` in `IconCountTest`. On an API 36 emulator without a window, notifications taken from `adb shell dumpsys notification`: on the previous build with only the "Today" count on, there was a notification of the channel `icon_count`; after the new build was installed over it, before the app was even started, the notification shade holds one notification with tasks in the channel with a count, the count is 2, the notification with tasks is on in the settings, the channel `icon_count` is deleted. With "Overdue tasks" chosen and no overdue tasks, and with "Do not show", the notification is in the channel without a badge, with no count. With the notification with tasks off, Lists has no notifications. Not verified: the heading "Overdue: N" on the emulator (only by a test); how the home screen launcher draws the badge; the caption under the choice in the settings on screen; the phone of the author of the project.

**Clearing "Completed"** (R46) — the core is verified by the tests `r46_*`, `s25_clearing_completed_reaches_the_other_device_and_outlives_a_reopen` and `c6_cleared_completed_tasks_leave_the_server`, the operation of the web server by the test `a_task_from_quick_entry_to_the_trash`. In the web (a browser without a window) and on the Android emulator it was walked through in full: choosing the age, declining the confirmation, clearing. On macOS it was checked that "Completed" opens with the action "Clear…"; the menu itself and the confirmation were not checked by pressing.

**Hiding "Completed"** (R47) — macOS only. Verified in a hidden instance of the debug build: with the setting off, the sidebar has one row less, an open "Completed" is replaced by "Inbox", ⌘5 does nothing; after a restart the setting is kept, and the menu item "Go → Completed" is unavailable; with the setting on, everything comes back. Not checked: the switch in the settings window by pressing, and the change of availability of the menu item right after switching, without a restart — the menu bar of a hidden app is not updated, so the action of the item also checks the setting itself. The list switch "Keep completed tasks in this list" (R2) is verified by the test `r2_list_settings_are_stored_and_applied`, including turning it off.

**Spacing between tasks** (R122, #256) — the decisions of the author of the project of 2026-10-14: three steps on both platforms, "Normal" by default; on macOS "Normal" was matched against a snapshot of Things 3 (a row of about 30 points), on Android 48, 52 and 60 dp without shrinking the touch area of the mark; the setting is kept on the device. Before #256 a row on macOS was 40 points (51 with the list name): the height was set by the 20-point chevron, not by the text. macOS was verified on 2026-10-14 in a hidden instance of the debug build with a separate data folder: the steps `spacing` and `rowheights` of the debug script give 26, 30 and 40 points in "Inbox", 41, 45 and 55 in "Today"; the choice survives a restart; the "General" tab drawn by the step `settingsshot` shows "Spacing between tasks" in the group "Text". Android: the tests `r122_*` in `RowSpacingTest`; on a separate emulator `shareding_api28` without a window, through `uiautomator`, rows of 48, 52 and 60 dp in "Inbox" and in "Today", choosing "Compact" in the settings changes the list at once, the mark works in a "Compact" row. Not verified: the rows in a visible macOS window, the choice in the macOS settings window by pressing, other text sizes.

**Hidden Inbox** (R121, R40, S32, #255) — decisions of the author of the project of 2026-10-14: the hidden flag and the default list for new tasks are synced; hiding asks for a list, moves the open tasks if chosen and switches the default list to the last used one; the row comes back while Inbox has open tasks; it cannot be hidden without other lists; both platforms. The core is checked by the tests `r121_*` and `r40_*` in `core/tests/store.rs`, `s32_hidden_inbox_and_the_default_list_reach_the_other_device` in `core/tests/sync.rs` and `r121_tasks_without_a_list_go_to_the_default_list_while_inbox_is_hidden` in `core/tests/import.rs`. macOS was checked on 2026-10-14 in a hidden instance of the debug build with a separate data folder, with the `hideinbox` step of the debug script: moving two tasks to "Home" — there is no Inbox row, "Home" is open with the moved tasks, the default list is "Last used list"; undo (the `undo` step) brings back the task and the row; "Hide Without Moving" — the row is visible with its count, also after a restart; the former choice of list on the device, passed as a launch argument, is written to the shared settings. The "General" tab is drawn by the `settingsshot` step: the switch stands first in "Sidebar", and Inbox is not among the items of R93. Android was checked on 2026-10-14 on a separate emulator `shareding_api28` without a window through `uiautomator`: the dialog with the number of tasks and the lists, "Move and hide" closes the settings and shows "Home" with a notice, "Undo" brings back the tasks and the row, "Hide without moving" keeps the row, and the choice of the default list has no Inbox. Not checked: the hide dialog on macOS on the screen, ⌘Z by hand in a visible window, "Share" on macOS, sync between real devices.

**Items of the sidebar** (R93, #151) — decisions of the author of the project of 2026-10-09 are recorded in #151. The counts for "All", "Completed" and "Won't do" are checked by the test `r93_counts_say_which_built_in_views_hold_something` in `core/tests/store.rs`. macOS was checked on 2026-10-09 in a hidden instance of the debug build with a separate data folder; the rows are taken by the `sidebarshow` step, the number of table rows by the `sidebar` step. Empty install: four rows, ⌘5 leaves Inbox. After "won't do" on a task: two more rows, ⌘5 opens "Completed", ⌘6 opens "Won't do". A task brought back to work from "Won't do": four rows again, Inbox is open, ⌘6 does nothing. "Trash" — "Always", "Today" — "When it holds something", "All" — "Never": the rows Inbox, "Upcoming", "Trash"; ⌘4 does nothing, after "Always" it opens "All", after "Never" the app leaves the open "All" for Inbox. The items of the "Go" menu are not greyed out on purpose: in a hidden instance an item that was unavailable at launch stayed unavailable after the condition went away — the former setting R47 behaved the same way on a build before the fix. This was not checked in a visible window. The setting is kept in the user defaults shared by the installed app (`sidebarShow`); the entry was removed after the check. The migration of the former setting was checked with three launches with "Show Completed" turned off and a "won't do" task: in the first there are no "Completed" and "Won't do" rows, also after choosing "Always" for "Trash"; in the second they are still absent, and after choosing "When it holds something" for "Completed" only that row appears; in the third the choice is kept. Not checked: the settings section on the screen and choosing in it, a visible window.

**Completed tasks stay in view** (R68) — the core is checked by the tests `r68_*` and `s32_the_shared_setting_merges_like_any_field_and_reaches_a_new_device`, the web server by the test `a_completed_task_stays_in_view_until_the_setting_says_otherwise`. Checked on 2026-10-07. macOS, a hidden instance of the debug build, the `keepdone` and `rows` steps of the debug script: a completed task stays between its neighbours; with the setting "at once" it disappears, with the setting restored it appears again; with one minute it disappears by itself, with no action. Android, an API 36 emulator without a window, English interface, `uiautomator dump` and a screenshot: after a tap on the mark the row stays in place, the mark has a tick, the title is struck through and dimmed, there is no snackbar; a second tap brings the task back to work; the settings offer a choice of five values; with one minute the row disappeared by itself (completed at 17:11:50, gone at 17:14:23); with "at once" the row disappears and the "Undo" snackbar appears. Not checked: the macOS settings window and the look of the row on macOS by eye, the web interface in a browser, sync of the setting between real devices, the "Undo" button in the snackbar with "at once" after this fix, devices in different time zones (such a task may disappear at once: the completion time is stored without a zone).

**"Won't do"** (R69) — the core is checked by the tests `r69_*`, `s33_the_wont_do_outcome_merges_with_completion_and_survives_an_unaware_version` and `c27_*`, the web server by the test `r69_a_task_is_closed_as_wont_do_and_listed_in_its_view`. Checked on 2026-10-08. macOS, a hidden instance of the debug build in a separate data folder, the `wont`, `task`, `go` and `rows` steps of the debug script: the marked task stays between its neighbours and reads as "won't do"; it is in "Won't do" and in "Completed"; a tap on the mark brings it back to work, and it disappears from "Won't do"; a completed task does not get into "Won't do"; with the setting "at once" the marked task disappears from Inbox and is visible in both logs; the "Won't do" item in the "Go" menu is present and available. Android, an API 36 emulator without a window, English interface, `uiautomator dump` and a screenshot: the long-press menu has "Won't do"; after it the row stays in place, the mark shows a cross in a square, the title is struck through and dimmed; in the panel of lists "Won't do" stands under "Completed", and the task is visible in both views; a tap on the mark brings it back to work; the "Won't do" item in the "⋮" menu of the card closes the task. Not checked: the context menu of the row, the chip in the card, the look of the mark and ⌘6 on macOS by eye and by keystrokes; hiding the "Won't do" row with the setting R47; the web interface in a browser (only the syntax of the page script and the server operations were checked); the Russian interface on Android; the "Undo" snackbar with the setting "at once"; a repeating task and subtasks in the interfaces (checked in the core); exchange with a real CalDAV server and with a third-party client; a device with an earlier version of the app — its behaviour is derived from the code and reproduced in the tests by writing a single field.

**Subtask menu and undo of a close** (R11, R38, R69, #201) — found on 2026-10-10 on macOS: "Won't do" in the menu over a subtask closed the whole task, and ⌘Z brought back only that task. There are two causes, both reproduced in a hidden instance of the debug build before the fix. The first: SwiftUI gives the context menu of a list row to the whole row, and subtasks are drawn inside the row of the parent — the menu over a subtask was the menu of the parent (it had the "Make it a project" item, which a subtask does not have), and the due date, priority, move and deletion went to the parent in the same way. Now an AppKit view marks the place of a subtask, and a right click or ⌃click on it opens the menu of the subtask, built from the same items; over the text and over a file their own menus remain. The second: undo brought back to work one task; "Undo" on Android and in the web worked the same way. Now undo calls `undo_close_task` of the core: the subtasks closed by the same action come back to work too — they have the same moment of closing, written in the same millisecond. The core is checked by the tests `r69_r11_undoing_a_close_reopens_the_subtasks_closed_with_it` and `r69_undoing_the_close_of_a_subtask_leaves_its_parent_and_neighbours_alone`, the web server by the test `r69_r11_undo_brings_back_the_subtasks_closed_with_the_task`. macOS was checked with the `rowmenu` and `undo` steps of the debug script: the menu over a subtask and over a subtask of a subtask has no "Make it a project"; "Won't do" from it closes only that subtask and its subtasks; the menu over the title of the parent is as before; undo of "Won't do" and of the completion of the parent brings back to work the parent and both subtasks. The `rowmenu` step asks the views for the menu and does not open it. Not checked: a right click and a ⌃click with the mouse in a visible window — that the subtask menu opens at the place of the click, and that the text menu in the open card of a subtask stays its own; Android and the web in the interface (the "Undo" snackbar appears only with the setting "at once"; the Android build and unit tests passed).

**Time to the second** (R68) — noticed in 0.1.0-rc.8: with the setting "after 1 min" a completed task stayed in the list noticeably longer. Reproduced on 2026-10-07 in a hidden instance of the macOS debug build by polling the rows every 20 seconds: the task disappeared between the 140th and the 160th second. There are two causes: the completion time was stored to the minute, and the app checked the rows once a minute (Android every 30 seconds, the web every 20). Now the time is counted from the write stamp of the `done` field, and the app waits exactly until the nearest row leaves. The core is checked by the test `r68_the_time_runs_to_the_second_and_the_store_tells_when_it_ends`; an import with a task completed long ago is still covered by the test `microsoft_to_do_lists_json`. After the fix, with one minute: on macOS (hidden instance, polling every 5 seconds) the row is in place at the 55th second and gone by the 60th; on an Android API 36 emulator without a window it is in place at the 55th and gone by the 59th. Not checked: the web interface in a browser, a sleeping Mac and a phone with the screen off, devices in different time zones.

**Seconds and "at the end of the day"** (R68, S32) — the core is checked by the tests `r68_*`, among them `r68_until_the_end_of_the_day_keeps_what_was_completed_today_until_midnight`, by the tests `s32_the_time_is_stored_so_that_a_version_that_knows_only_minutes_can_read_it` and `s32_the_shared_setting_merges_like_any_field_and_reaches_a_new_device`; the web server by the test `a_completed_task_stays_in_view_until_the_setting_says_otherwise`; the choice on Android by the test `KeepDoneChoicesTest`. Checked on 2026-10-08. macOS, a hidden instance of the debug build on a new data folder, the `keepdone` and `rows` steps of the debug script: without the setting 5 seconds apply, a completed task is in place at the 4th second and gone by itself by the 6.5th; with "at the end of the day" it appears again, with "at once" it disappears. Android, an API 36 emulator without a window, English interface, app data cleared, `uiautomator dump`: the settings show "after 5 s", the choice has four values — "at once", "after 5 s", "after 15 s", "at the end of the day"; a completed task disappeared by itself between the 4th and the 6th second, there is no snackbar; with "at the end of the day" it came back to its place; with the emulator clock set to 23:59:40, both completed tasks are in place at 23:59:57 and gone by themselves by 00:00:01. The choice in the web interface: the `keepChoices` function of the page was run on its own in node — four values, and the time in effect in minutes comes fifth. Not checked: the macOS settings window by eye, the Russian labels on the screen, the web interface in a browser, sync of the setting between real devices, a device with a version that knows only minutes (its behaviour is derived from the code: `"5s"` does not parse as a number), a clock change and the switch to daylight saving time with "at the end of the day", a sleeping Mac and a phone with the screen off at midnight, devices in different time zones: the day is counted by the device clock, so a task completed in another zone may disappear at once.

**Repeating tasks stay in view, and their completion is undone** (R68, R17, R128, S44, #287) — noticed by the author of the project on 2026-10-15 in 0.4.0: with the setting "after 5 s" a repeating task disappeared from "Today" at once. The cause is the former wording of R68: the task moved on to the next repeat, and the record of the repeat did not get into the views. Decisions of the author of the project of 2026-10-15 are recorded in #287: the record stands in the place of the task, the task is at its new date at once, undo brings back the dates, the reminder, the remaining repeats and the subtasks completed earlier, only while the task is at the date this completion moved it to; undo is synced like ordinary edits. Chosen during the implementation and not decided in #287: undo does not delete the record but removes the completion from it, so completing the same repeat again brings back the same record (S7); the record of a project task stands in the project and in "Today", but not as a list row; a record made by an earlier version, without the `undo` field, cannot be undone. The core is checked by the tests `r68_a_repeating_task_moves_on_and_its_record_stays_in_place_for_the_time`, `r68_the_record_of_a_project_task_stands_in_the_project_only`, `r128_*`, `r17_*` and `s44_undo_of_a_completed_repeat_reaches_the_other_device`, the web server by the test `r128_the_completion_of_a_repeat_is_taken_back`, the selection of several tasks on Android by `r128_completing_a_repeating_task_is_taken_back_too`. macOS was checked on 2026-10-15 in a hidden instance of the debug build with a separate data folder, a task "every day" due today, the setting "after 15 s": after the `done` step "Today" shows the completed record; `undo` brings the task back to today, open; Inbox shows the task and the record, and the `done` step on the record brings back the task; after 17 seconds the record is not in "Today", it is in "Completed". Android was checked on 2026-10-15 on the emulator `shareding_api36` without a window through `uiautomator` and a screenshot: with the setting "after 30 s" the record is struck through in place, the mark is active, the message about the move has no "Undo", a tap on the mark brings back the task, after 31 seconds the record is gone; with the setting "at once" the message has "Undo", and it brings the task back to the former date. The web was checked on 2026-10-15 in headless Chromium through Playwright on a local server: the same steps with the mark of the record and with "Undo" at "at once". Not checked: ⌘Z and a tap on the mark by hand in a visible macOS window, the look of the struck-through record on macOS on the screen, sync of undo between real devices and through CalDAV.

**CalDAV** — the second way to sync, rules in [caldav.md](caldav.md); it is chosen in the settings on both platforms. Checked by core tests against a test server; it was not run against a real server or from the interface of the apps.

**Web** — the `lists-web` server with the same core: lists, tasks with subtasks, dates, priority, repeats by preset, tags, attachments, search, trash; sign-in with a password. Checked by the tests `web/tests/api.rs` and by a run: the page was opened in Chrome at desktop and phone size, and the data was synced with macOS and Android through a local WebDAV server. Screenshots of all three platforms are in `docs/screenshots/`.

**Entry and editing** (R37–R44, several reminders from R36) — macOS was checked with a debug script that sends keystrokes and clicks to the app from inside (`apple/App/DebugScript.swift`, only in the debug build), with window snapshots: the card on ⌘N with all fields, a multi-line note in the card, Return and ⌥Return in the note in both modes, finishing with Esc, ⌘D from the note opens the calendar with focus in it, the quick entry window with the same card and a date picker, which does not close the window, focus in the quick entry window while the app is active. The first showing of the quick entry window was checked with the `quicktrace` step of the same script: the size of the window and the place of the card in it are the same from the first frame and do not differ from later showings; the first showing was not checked by eye on the screen. Keyboard editing (R44) was checked with the same script in a hidden window: ↓, Return, typing, Esc — the title changed, the card is closed, the keyboard is at the list, ↓ moves the selection on; Tab from the title moves to the note, Esc in the note closes the card; after expanding with the chevron the keyboard stays at the list, and Esc closes the card. A click inside the open card after editing the title was checked with the same script and the `ghost` step, which gives the click to a hidden window: before the fix (#86) the title was saved, the keyboard moved to the list, the card stayed open and Esc did nothing; after it Esc closes the card, the row stays selected, a second Esc changes nothing. A double click on a row and Esc after a click in the card of a subtask were not checked. Tab from the note through the chips (R62) was checked with the same script in a hidden app, with the system "Keyboard navigation" turned off: from the title Tab goes to the note, the next one to the start chip, then on through due date, priority, tags and "+", after the last one to the title; ⇧Tab on the first chip goes back to the note; ⌥Tab inserts a tab into the note; Space on the start chip and Return on the due chip open their pickers (judged by the state of the card: a hidden app does not show the picker itself), Space on a tag chip removes the tag and does not finish the task, Esc on a chip closes the card and gives the keyboard back to the list. The same in the card of a new task: start, due date, repeat, priority, tag, list, file, then the title. Esc with an open picker was checked with the same script and the `ghost` step: it puts the windows of the hidden app on the screen transparent, without keyboard and mouse, so the picker opens as a real window and the keystrokes go to it. Before the fix one Esc closed both the picker and the card; after it the first Esc closes the start picker opened with Space on the chip, the card stays open and the keyboard returns to the chip, and the second closes the card. The same with the due date picker opened with ⌘D from the note (the keyboard returns to the note), and in the card of a new task with the start, repeat and tag pickers; the second Esc there creates the task. The app is not active during this, and the picker window does not become key: the script gives the keystrokes to it directly. Not checked with a real keyboard in an active app. Not checked: the chip outline by eye, the priority, list and "+" menus opened from the keyboard, the file picker, Esc with an open reminder or tag picker in an open task (both open from the "+" menu) and in the quick entry window, and the card in the quick entry window. The date picker keys (R39) were checked with the same script in a hidden app on a date editor built in a window that is not shown (the `dateeditor`, `code`, `datefield` steps): T, M, W, the digits of the main and the numeric keypad, "+" and "−", ⌫ with and without a date, keeping the time when a day is chosen; with the keyboard at the calendar the keys work, with the keyboard at the time field they do not. The keystrokes are given by key code without a character, so the layout plays no part in the check. In the card itself the keys did not work at first: a keystroke from the keyboard comes with the window the picker hangs on, not with the picker window, and the interceptor let it through; for the same reason Return did not reach the "Done" button. The earlier check did not see this: the `code` step put the picker window into the keystroke. Found with keystrokes that `scripts/postkey.swift` sends to the process from outside the way the keyboard driver sends them (`CGEvent.postToPid`), in a hidden app with the `ghost` step: "→" moved the day, "+" and Return did nothing. After the fix, the same way: ⌘D, "+", "+", Return — the due date is in two days, the picker is closed. The `code` step now puts the same window into the keystroke as the keyboard does, and does not pass without the fix. It checked: ⌘D and M in the card of a new task, ⌘S and W, "+" and "−" of the main and the numeric keypad with the Enter of the numeric keypad, in an open task ⌘D and 3, then ⌘D and ⌫; with the keyboard at the time field T and 3 do not set a date, Return confirms. The app is not active in any of these checks. Not checked: a real keyboard in an active app, the Russian layout on it, a picker opened by a click on the chip; the button tooltips were not checked by eye. Not checked: quick entry focus while another app is active; the "Subtasks N/M" line on a task with subtasks; the choice of the default list; creating a filter with the "+" button. Android was checked on the emulator: the quick entry window with a note, a due date and a priority creates a task with these values, the editor shows "Start" and "Due", the subtask field appears from the "+" menu, the settings have the default list, the recognition switch and the choice of several reminders (the dialog opens; the final label after the choice was not observed — the system request for notification permission got in the way). Several reminders are checked by the core test `notifications_follow_reminders_due_dates_and_settings`.

**Repeat on creation** (R45) — macOS was checked with the debug script in a hidden window: the card of a new task with the due date 2026-10-05 and the preset "Every week" creates a task with that rule, and after completion the due date becomes 2026-10-12; "Every month" with a due date on the 31st remembers the day 31, that is, the rule is assigned after the dates. The script sets the fields of the card directly, as its popovers do. Not checked: a click on the "Repeat" chip and ⇧⌘R in the card of a new and of an open task — the popover is not shown while the app window is hidden. Android was checked on the emulator: in the quick entry window the "Repeat" chip opens the repeat picker, after choosing "Every week" the chip shows the rule, and the created task keeps it in the database.

**The add button on Android** (R57) — checked on an emulator without a window through `uiautomator dump` and by reading the app database: the card opens above the keyboard with the cursor in the title; "Done" creates the task, and the card stays open and empty; a task with a note, the due date "tomorrow" and high priority is created in one pass; the due date from "Today" and the tag from a tag view, checked at the same time, were dropped in R75; in a project it becomes a subtask, the card has no list choice, `!!` in the title gives the priority; in a list view the card shows that list, and the task goes into it; after "Add" from the note typing goes into the title; a tap outside the card and "Back" close it; in "Completed" and in search there is no button. The quick entry window with the same card creates a task with a note and a priority from the title and closes. Not checked: "Trash" (it had no tasks), repeat and files from the card on the main screen, choosing another list in the card, the Russian interface. The Android app has no automated tests; the check is manual.

**The filled-in due date in the card on Android** (R57) — checked on 2026-10-08 on an API 36 emulator without a window, English interface; the values at the icons are taken from `uiautomator dump`, the tasks from the app database. The due date from the "Today" view, checked at the same time, was dropped in R75. In a list with the default due date "today" the card opens with "today": an untouched due date is saved, a removed one is not, although the list would fill it in; with a start date the due icon is empty, and the task gets no due date; `tomorrow` in the title wins over the due date of the list, and a due date chosen in the field wins over `tomorrow`. After choosing in the card a list without such a setting the due icon becomes empty. The rules for choosing the due date are covered by the tests `NewTaskDueTest`; `make android` runs them. Not checked: the quick entry window with a default list that has the due date "today"; a screen rotation after the due date was removed; the expanded card; the Russian interface; a real device.

**A view fills in nothing** (R75) — checked on 2026-10-08 with builds of the branch. Android: an API 28 emulator (1080×1920) without a window, English interface; the card from screenshots (`uiautomator dump` does not see the bottom sheet on this API), the tasks from the app database. In "Today" the card opens with an empty due date, and the task is created without a due date. In the view of the tag `home` the task is created without tags. In a list with the default high priority and due date "today" the card opens with "today" and "!!!" at the icons, and the task gets both values; `tomorrow !` in the title puts "tomorrow" and "!" at the icons, and the task gets tomorrow's due date and low priority; after "No priority" and "Remove" the icons are empty, the task is created without priority and due date, and the next card opens with the values of the list again; after choosing Inbox in the card both icons are empty. The rules are covered by the tests `NewTaskDueTest` (`r75_*`, `r41_*`). macOS: a hidden instance of the debug build with a separate data folder, the `go`, `mklist`, `draft`, `due`, `priority`, `start`, `draftlist` and `task` steps of the debug script; `draft` prints what the card puts at the icons, `task` prints the created task. In "Today" and in a tag view the card has no due date and no tags, and the tasks are created without them. In a list with the same defaults the card shows today's due date and high priority, and the task gets both; with the due date and priority removed, neither; `tomorrow !` in the title gives tomorrow's due date and low priority; the due date and priority chosen in the card win over `tomorrow !!!` in the title; with a start date the due icon is empty and the task does not get it, the priority stays; choosing Inbox in the card removes both values, choosing a list with values while in Inbox sets them. Web: the test `web/tests/api.rs` — `quickAdd` in "Today" returns a task without a due date, in a tag view without tags. Not checked: the cards on the macOS screen by eye and the quick entry window on both platforms; on Android, a priority chosen in the card against a priority from the title (only by the test `finalPriority`), the expanded card, a screen rotation, the Russian interface, a real device; the "New task" line in a browser.

**Screen rotation with the card on Android** (R63) — before the fix reproduced on an API 36 emulator without a window: the card with a typed title disappears after a rotation (`settings put system user_rotation 1`), and the list remains on the screen. The cause: the open-card flag and its fields were kept in `remember`, and the activity is created anew on rotation; now they are in `rememberSaveable`. After the fix it was checked in the same place through `uiautomator dump` and by reading the app database: a card with a title, a note, the due date "tomorrow", high priority and a custom repeat "every 2 weeks: Tue, Thu" survived a rotation both ways and back, and the task created after that keeps all these values; a file chosen in the card stayed after the rotation and is attached to the created task; the priority dialog and the custom repeat dialog with interval 2 stayed open; an empty card after "Add" stays empty, and one closed with "Cancel" does not appear again; the tag dialog in the task editor stayed open with the typed text; the quick entry window from "Share" kept the title added to it; the note from the clipboard is in place after the rotation together with its button, and one removed with the button did not come back. Not checked: the chosen weekdays in the custom repeat dialog during a rotation (the chip mark could not be read from `uiautomator dump`; the final rule after confirming is correct), the calendar and time steps of the date picker, the list choice, restoring after the system unloaded the app from memory, a real device. In landscape with the keyboard open the card shows only the title and the note — the chips and buttons end up beyond the edge; this was not looked into. This fix does not touch the list, filter and settings dialogs.

**Two sizes of the card of a new task on Android** (R64) — checked on 2026-10-07 on an API 36 emulator (1080×2400, 420 dpi) without a window, English interface, bounds taken from `uiautomator dump`, tasks from the app database. The compact card: with the due date "tomorrow", the repeat "Every week" and priority `!!!` all six icons and the list name fit the screen width (the right edge of the row at 1032 of 1080), the values are shown in full; when a start date is set as well, three long values are cut with an ellipsis, and the row does not go beyond the edge. A task with a note, a start date, a due date, a repeat and high priority was created in one pass from the compact card, a task with a note, a due date and a priority from the expanded one; after sending, the card stays open, empty and at the same size. Expanding and collapsing keep the title, the note and the field values, and the cursor stays in the note. In landscape the card is expanded, and there is no collapse button; with the keyboard open (its top at 394 of 1080) the title and the send button are visible, the note takes typing, and one can scroll to the "Due" line; after going back to portrait the card is compact again, and the title, the note and the due date are in place. "Back" closes the card. The quick entry window: the same compact and expanded views; in landscape a task with a note, a due date, a repeat and a priority was created, and the window closed. Not checked: the Russian interface (the labels are longer, cutting of values is more likely), the card in a project (a row without the list icon), the file picker and choosing another list from the new dialog, scrolling the fields in the quick entry window in landscape, a screen narrower than 360 dp, the look on a real device. The Android app has no automated tests; the check is manual.

**The note of the compact card grows to five lines on Android** (R64) — checked on 2026-10-09 on an API 36 emulator (1080×2400) without a window, English interface, dark appearance, bounds taken from `uiautomator dump`, the look from screenshots; the text was typed through `adb shell input`. Before the fix: after ten lines the note field has the same height as an empty one (147 px), only the tenth line is visible in it, and the beginning goes up out of view. After the fix: an empty note takes one line; with three lines the field is 243 px high, all three are visible, and the row of icons under them is in its former place above the keyboard; with ten, 349 px, the lines from the sixth to the tenth and the cursor are visible, the title and the send button are visible, and the card does not grow higher. Expanding shows all ten lines, collapsing returns the field to five lines, and typing after it goes to the end of the note. The quick entry window from "Share": the same 349 px field with ten lines, and the title, the send button and the row of icons are visible above the keyboard. The limit in lines is covered by the test `NoteLinesTest`; `make android` runs it. Not checked: typing on the on-screen keyboard, scrolling the note with a finger inside the field, a long line wrapped instead of Enter, a note from the clipboard (R58) longer than five lines, a screen rotation with a long note, a short window (split screen), a large system font, the Russian interface, a real phone.

**Note from the clipboard** (R58) — found out before the implementation (2026-10-06): in macOS SDK 27 `NSPasteboard` has `accessBehavior`, and according to the header programmatic reading of the general pasteboard should ask for permission by default; according to third-party write-ups the prompt is not enabled in released versions, and on macOS 26.6.2 a process without a bundle got `alwaysAllow`. If the system starts asking, the prompt will appear the first time the window opens, and the user's choice lives in the system settings; there is no handling of a refusal of our own — a refusal looks like an empty clipboard. On Android reading the clipboard description (`getPrimaryClipDescription`) does not cause a system message, and reading the content does (Android 12 documentation). macOS was checked with the debug script in a hidden window, the `copytext`, `copysecret`, `copyfile`, `clipnotes`, `quicknote` steps; the script writes to a separate named pasteboard (`LISTS_DEBUG_PASTEBOARD`), not to the general one: a copied address ends up in the note, opening again gives an empty note, the same text copied again is filled in again, spaces at the edges are dropped, a file, content marked `org.nspasteboard.ConcealedType` and a text of 2001 characters are skipped, 2000 characters are taken; with the setting off the note is empty, and after turning it on the same text is filled in, that is, the setting turned off does not touch the clipboard; the remove button is visible on the card snapshot. Not checked on macOS: reading the general pasteboard (the script does not touch it), a click on the remove button and the cursor in the title — a hidden window takes neither clicks nor the keyboard; whether the system prompt appears for a signed app. Android was checked on an API 36 emulator without a window through `uiautomator dump`: text copied in the app itself and text left in the clipboard by another app end up in the note of the window opened with `am start`, focus in the title; opening again gives an empty note; a tap on the remove button clears the note; with the setting off the note is empty and the used-text mark does not change; the window from "Share" takes its own text, and the copied text stays for the next opening; 2001 characters are skipped, 2000 are taken. Not checked on Android: a clipboard with the `IS_SENSITIVE` flag, a link without text (`text/uri-list`), the system message about reading itself, opening from the shortcut and from the tile (they start the same window without an action in the intent, like `am start`).

**Dialogs above the keyboard on Android** (R59) — the cause was established on an API 36 emulator without a window by comparing the bounds from `uiautomator dump` with the keyboard frame from `dumpsys window`: with the keyboard open the settings dialog kept its height and moved up, the title ended up under the status bar, and "User name", "Password" and the buttons under the keyboard (its top at 1517, the fields at 1517–1853, the buttons at 1967); a tap at their coordinates hit the keyboard, and typing went into "Address". In the new list dialog the "Create" button was under the keyboard. After the fix, in the same place: the settings, list and filter dialogs are entirely above the keyboard, the buttons at 1302–1355; in the settings after scrolling a tap on "User name" and "Password" moves the focus, and the typed text goes into its own field; without the keyboard the settings dialog takes its former place. Not checked: a finger on a device; a tap on a field of which only a narrow strip is visible above the keyboard did not move the focus — the field has to be scrolled into the visible part first. Landscape was checked on 2026-10-07 in the same place (window 2400×1080, 420 dpi, English interface). Before the fix reproduced on the new list dialog: after a tap on the field the screen tree keeps the title and two empty button areas, and there is no field. After the fix the list, filter, settings, tag and custom repeat dialogs take the whole screen; with the keyboard open (its top at 394, at 502 for the numeric one) the title and the buttons stand at 64–190, the content scrolls between them and the keyboard, and the field keeps focus and takes typing. A list and a filter were created with the "Create" button, a tag was added with the "Add" button, a custom repeat with 7 repeats was saved with the "Done" button; in the settings the text typed in "Address" and "User name" went into their own fields, and "Close" closed the dialog without saving. The tag dialog with typed text survived a rotation to portrait and became an ordinary dialog there; in portrait the tag and settings dialogs with the keyboard stand as before, the settings buttons at 1302–1355 with the keyboard from 1517. Not checked in landscape: the "Password" field and saving the settings, editing an existing list and filter, the Russian interface (the settings buttons "Import…", "Close", "Save and sync" took 1744–2336 of 2400 in width; longer labels may squeeze the title), a tablet and a foldable phone (a window taller than 480 dp, the dialog is ordinary there), a real device. The Android app has no automated tests; the check is manual.

**Swipe from the left edge on Android** (R65) — checked on 2026-10-07 on an API 36 emulator (1080×2400, 420 dpi) without a window, gesture navigation, English interface; the gestures were given through `adb shell input swipe`, the result taken from `uiautomator dump`. Before the fix: a swipe to the right started 42 dp from the edge over a task completed it, and the panel did not open.
