After: one started at 42 dp and at 53 dp opens the panel, and the task is intact; one started at 61 dp completes the task and shows "Undo"; in the strip a tap on the circle, a long press (menu) and scrolling work; a swipe to the left opens the due date picker; one started at 8 dp is taken by the system back gesture. Not checked: a finger on a real device, three-button navigation, a tablet.

**Exact alarms on Android** (R66) — checked on 2026-10-07 on an API 36 emulator without a window, English interface; the alarms taken from `adb shell dumpsys alarm`, the screen from `uiautomator dump`. Without the permission: the alarm at 09:00 the next day has a delivery window of one hour (`window=+1h`), the notification settings have the line "Reminders may come late" with a hint, and a tap opens the system screen "Alarms & reminders" for Lists. After the permission is turned on on that screen the alarm is rescheduled without starting the app: `window=0`, `exactAllowReason=permission`; the line disappeared from the settings. After the permission is turned off there, the system stopped the app and removed its alarms; after the next launch the alarm is set again with a window of an hour. Not checked: the notification itself at the set minute on a sleeping device, Android 12 and older, a real phone, the Russian interface; the return of alarms by background sync after the permission is revoked — a conclusion from the code, not a check.

**Reminders as alarms on Android** (R119, #207) — decision of the author of the project of 2026-10-14: a switch in "Notifications", off by default; the option "always as an alarm" was rejected because of the icon that the system is entitled to show permanently. The choice of the way is checked by the tests `r119_*` in `AlarmWayTest`. The cause was established on 2026-10-14 on the phone of the author: OnePlus CPH2747, Android 16, alarms from `adb shell dumpsys alarm`, delivery from the system log (`sending alarm`). The system sets the exact alarm of Lists (`setExactAndAllowWhileIdle`, `exact=true` in the build log) with the window of an inexact one: 75 % of the time to the due moment, no more than an hour, with the flags `0x8`; the same with the reason `allow-listed` and with the reason `permission`, from the background and from the foreground, and on a fresh install with one alarm. A separate app with the same code under the name `org.evsyukov.alarmprobe` got exact alarms with window 0, and under the name `org.evsyukov.quietcheck` got windows for `setExactAndAllowWhileIdle`, `setExact` and `setWindow` with window 0; only `setAlarmClock` kept window 0: sent at 09:58:00.004, the others at 09:58:18. By what sign the firmware tells the apps apart is not established; the package name is a guess. A test copy of Lists with the switch: off — window `+8m38s`; on — the alarm is rescheduled at once, window 0, and the system recorded it as the next alarm; on a locked phone it was sent at 10:30:00.004, the notification at 10:30:00.030 (and at 10:13:00.008 / 10:13:00.024). There is no alarm icon in the status bar and no time on the lock screen on this phone. Not checked: the look on a Pixel and in other skins, a tap on the time of the next alarm, a phone left asleep longer than a few minutes, Android earlier than 16.

**Appearance** (R61) — checked with a light system. macOS: a hidden instance of the debug build, the `appearance` step of the debug script — after choosing "Dark" the main window and the quick entry window are dark at once, after a restart the choice is kept and the windows are dark, "Light" and "Same as the system" bring back light ones; the settings window and the menu bar window were not checked: the hidden app does not open them. Android: an emulator without a window in light mode — after choosing "Dark" in the settings the settings dialog, the main screen with the status bar icons and the quick entry window became dark, and after an app restart the screen stayed dark. Web: a headless browser against a local server — choosing "Dark" sets the `dark` class, a dark background and light text, and is kept after a page reload; with a dark system choosing "Light" gives a light page and also survives a reload; "Same as the system" removes the entry from `localStorage`. Not checked: "Light" with a dark system on macOS and Android.

**Square mark** — the completion mark is drawn as a square on macOS, Android and in the web: in the task row, in the card, in the menu bar window; the icon of the "Completed" view and the icon under the right swipe are square too. Checked on 2026-10-07: on an Android API 36 emulator without a window by a screenshot of "Today" — the rows have a square mark; on macOS a hidden instance of the debug build starts, creates and completes a task. Not checked by eye: the look of the mark on macOS and in the web, the mark of a completed task and the card on Android.

**Mark aligned to the first line on macOS** — noticed in 0.1.0-rc.8: the square mark in the task row stands above the title. The cause was established by measurement: the mark is larger than the capital letter of the title (`title3` against `body`) and stood on the baseline of the text, so its middle was above the middle of the capital by 0.6–1.1 pt, the more the larger the text. The round mark before the switch to the square stood the same way: a separate program with the same layout showed 0.7–1.2 pt. After the fix the middle of the mark is tied to the middle of the capital letter of the first line. Checked on 2026-10-08 in a hidden instance of the debug build with the `rowshot` step of the debug script: it draws the row of the selected task to a file, eight pixels per point, and the size and design for one run are set by the launch arguments `-textScale` and `-fontDesign`, without changing the settings. By the pixels of the snapshot the middle of the mark is no more than 0.06 pt from the middle of the letter "H" at all five sizes of the system font, with the three other designs at the regular size and with serifs at the largest; before the fix the same snapshots gave from 0.62 to 1.12 pt. The row measured had a due date under the title; the row without it was measured at size 1.15, with the same result. The top of the mark stays above the top of the letter by 2–3.3 pt: the mark is larger than the letter. Not checked: the row in the list itself on the screen (a hidden window does not draw rows, and the step draws the same row apart from the list), the expanded card with the title field, the mark of a completed task by pixels (on the snapshot it is in the same place), the look by eye.

**Sync by gesture on Android** (R67) — checked 2026-10-07 on a windowless API 36 emulator against the server from `core/examples/webdav.rs`, English interface; the gestures were sent through `adb shell input swipe`, the result was taken from `uiautomator dump` and screenshots. A second device added a task and uploaded it: three seconds later without the gesture the task is not on the screen, three seconds after a swipe down it is. During the gesture an indicator is visible at the top, and it disappears after sync; in an empty list the gesture works the same way. Swiping a row to the right and swiping from the left edge work as before. With sync turned off there is no indicator. Before the fix the sync icon and the gesture stayed on the screen after sync was turned off in the settings until the app was restarted; now they disappear at once. Not checked: a finger on a real device, the gesture during a sync in progress, a network error, CalDAV.

**Capital letter on the Android keyboard** (R70) — noticed in 0.1.0-rc.9 on a phone: the title of a new task started with a lowercase letter. Checked 2026-10-08 on a windowless API 36 emulator: with the field focused, the `inputType` it gave the keyboard was taken from `adb shell dumpsys input_method`. Before the fix the title and the note, both in the card of a new task and in the editor, gave `0x8001` and `0x28001`: the autocorrect flag is there, the flag for a capital letter at the start of a sentence (`TYPE_TEXT_FLAG_CAP_SENTENCES`, `0x4000`) is not. After the fix the title and the note in the card and in the editor, a subtask, the name of a list and the name of a filter give `0xc001` (the note gives `0x2c001`); a tag, search and the filter fields "Tags" and "Contains" stay at `0x8001`. Autocorrect in the editor's note, which sits on a `BasicTextField` with the shown text substituted, is requested the same way as in the other fields. Before the fix the server address, the user name and the password also gave the autocorrect flag (the address `0x8011`, the password `0x8081`), after it they give `0x11`, `0x1` and `0x81`. Not checked: the address and the token of the notification server (the same field settings, not brought onto the screen); the capital letter itself while typing. The emulator keyboard puts no capital letter in any field, including the system field for a contact name, which asks for a capital in every word, so the behaviour with the keyboard setting on and off is left to be checked on a phone.

**Attachment content separate from the fields** (R20, R76, S34, S35) — the core is checked by the tests `s13_*`, `s34_*` and `s35_*` in `core/tests/sync.rs`, `s34_uploaded_content_nudges_the_others_once_more` in `core/tests/push.rs` and `two_devices_converge_through_webdav` in `core/tests/webdav.rs`; the storage in them is a folder and a local WebDAV server, and the files are tens of bytes. macOS was checked 2026-10-08 with a build of the branch started hidden (`open -g -j`, windows transparent through the `ghost` step) with a separate data folder and sync through a folder; the second device was the command `cargo run --example lists`, and the state was taken from the `files` and `rows` steps of the script and from the database. While a file lay in the storage in place of the `blobs` directory, the second device uploaded the fields and reported one waiting attachment; after sync the app showed the task, the attachment was waiting, the count of waiting attachments was one, and there was no sync error in the database. After the obstacle was removed and the second device uploaded the content, the `showfile:0` step (the same as a click on the name) downloaded the file, the count became zero, and the preview was requested once; the next sync brought the record of a second attachment and its content. Not checked on macOS: how the row and the icon look on the screen, the labels "downloading…" and "could not download", the update of an open card when the content was brought by a background pass, WebDAV with a real server, a file of tens of megabytes, the Russian interface. Android is built, `make android` with unit tests passes; it was not started on the emulator or on a device. The time of a pass with a real attachment was not measured: the content is still read into memory whole, and a request to WebDAV is limited to 120 seconds. The web is checked by the test `r76_a_waiting_attachment_is_fetched_when_it_is_asked_for` in `web/tests/api.rs` and on 2026-10-08 in headless Chromium (playwright-cli) on a server with sync through a folder; the second device was the command `cargo run --example lists`. While a file lay in place of the `blobs` directory, the sync button showed "Attachments waiting to sync: 1", the attachment row showed "not downloaded yet", and after a click "could not download, try later" with the reason in the tooltip. After the obstacle was removed and the second device uploaded the content, a second click downloaded a text file and opened a PNG over the page; the row stopped being waiting, and the button showed "Synced". Not checked in the web: what brought the file in the successful attempt — the request or the server's own pass, which the folder starts in the same seconds (the order is fixed only by the test); the label "downloading…" on the screen; how the row looks by eye; the English interface; WebDAV; a large file — while it downloads the request takes one of the four server threads.

**Viewing attachments** (R54–R56) — the web is checked by the test `r56_only_listed_types_are_shown_in_place` and in headless Chrome 154: a PNG has a thumbnail in the card, a click on the name opens the picture over the page, Esc closes it, a PDF is shown by the browser's built-in viewer inside a frame with `sandbox`; `.svg` and `.html` stay download links. Not opened in Safari and Firefox: if their PDF viewer does not work under `sandbox`, the "Download" link in the same window remains. macOS is checked by the `files` step of the debug script in a hidden window: the copies are created under the original names, the system sees `public.png`, `com.adobe.pdf`, `public.svg-image` and `public.html` in them, and the PNG thumbnail is read. The keys on a file row are checked by the same script in the hidden app (the steps `attach`, `showfile`, `preview`, `files`): after a file is shown the keyboard is at its row, Space asks Quick Look to show the file again and does not complete the task, ↓ does not move the selection, Esc closes the card and returns the keyboard to the list, after which ↓ and Space again apply to the selected row. Before the fix the same script completed the task with Space. Not checked: the Quick Look panel itself on a click and on Space, paging through files in it, the context menu and opening in the default app — they show a window over the others; the hidden app does not open the panel, so it is also not checked that the keyboard stays at the file row after the real panel is closed with Esc. Android is checked on a windowless emulator through `uiautomator dump` and screenshots: a thumbnail for a PNG, the picture full screen, zoom by double tap, a PDF as a page of full width, `.svg` goes to an external app, closing with the button. Not checked: pinch zoom, moving a zoomed picture, a multi-page PDF and a PDF with a password, a photo with a rotation in EXIF, HEIC.

**Markdown in notes** (R48–R53) — parsing and editing on Return are checked by the core tests `core/tests/markdown.rs` (`r48_*`, `r50_*`, `r52_*`, `r53_only_web_and_mail_addresses_are_links`). macOS is checked by the debug script in a hidden window, the steps `notes`, `caret`, `box` and `shot`: a note without focus is shown without markup, with the cursor the markup is visible only in its block, a click on a checkbox changes `[ ]` to `[x]` in the note, Return continues a bulleted and a numbered list and removes the marker from an empty item, text typed with keys lies in the core unchanged, the typeface from the settings (serif) applies to the note; the look was compared on snapshots of the field. Android is checked on a windowless emulator through `uiautomator dump` and screenshots: the formatting of the note, a tap on a checkbox without entering editing, `-` in the item with the cursor, Return in a list, the text in the database after edits. The web is checked by the test `r53_a_note_comes_with_the_ranges_to_show_it_as_markdown` and in headless Chrome: the note is formatted with text nodes, `<img onerror>` stayed text, there is no link with `javascript:`, a click opens a field with the source text, losing focus returns the formatted view. Opening links is checked separately, see R71 below. Not checked: a mouse click on a checkbox in a visible macOS window — the script calls the same handler, but not a mouse event; typing through the system input with composed characters (Japanese, diacritics by long press); changing the text size on the fly; wrapping of long list items; a quote and a code block inside a list on the devices; the macOS dark appearance; the card of a new task and the quick entry window on macOS, where the same field is used. Tables were not laid out as a grid then (done separately, R60), and the Android quick entry window was left a plain field.

**Links in a note** (R71) — checked 2026-10-08. macOS: a hidden instance of the debug build, the `noteclick` step of the debug script: it sends a click to the same handler as the mouse and prints the address that would go to the system, and the browser does not open. The note `см. https://example.org и [сайт](https://example.com/a)` ("see https://example.org and [site](https://example.com/a)"): without focus a click on the bare address gives `https://example.org`, on the word «сайт» ("site") — `https://example.com/a`, on «см.» ("see") — nothing; with the keyboard in the note and the markup visible — the same, and the cursor stays in place, also after a character is typed. Android: a windowless API 36 emulator, English interface, gestures through `adb shell input`, the browser launch taken from `logcat` (the line `START … act=android.intent.action.VIEW dat=https://example.org/`). A tap on a link in a note without focus opens Chrome, and the field does not get focus; a tap on a link with the keyboard in the note opens Chrome, and after returning a typed letter goes where the cursor was; a tap on a word next to it opens nothing and places the cursor; a long press on a link selects the whole address and opens nothing; a drag started on a link opens nothing; a link just typed opens with a tap. Not checked: a mouse click on a visible macOS window — the script calls the handler, not a mouse event, so the pointing-hand cursor, a drag from a link, a Shift-click and a double click are not checked; the switch to the browser itself on macOS; the card of a new task and the quick entry window on macOS, where the same field is used; a finger on a real phone; a `mailto:` link. Noticed along the way: a bare address typed on Android at the start of a line starts with a capital (`Https://…`, R70) and did not become a link — fixed separately, see below.

**Case of the scheme in a link address** (R48) — noticed 2026-10-08 while checking R71: in a note with the lines `Https://example.com/typed` and `x https://example.net/t` only the second was a link. The core is checked by the test `r48_the_scheme_of_a_link_is_read_without_regard_to_case` in `core/tests/markdown.rs`: a bare address, an address in angle brackets and a link in brackets with the scheme in any case are links, the scheme in the address is written in lowercase, the rest as typed; before the fix the test failed at the very first comparison. Android was checked 2026-10-08 on a windowless API 36 emulator. First without the app: `adb shell am start -a android.intent.action.VIEW -d 'Https://example.com/typed'` answers `unable to resolve Intent`, the same address with a lowercase scheme opens Chrome — so the core normalises the scheme, not each app. Then in the app: the note `Https://example.com/typed`, typed through `adb shell input text`, is shown as a link in the task editor, and a tap on it gives the line `START … act=android.intent.action.VIEW dat=https://example.com/…` in `logcat` and Chrome on top. Not checked: macOS and the web on the screen — they get the same ranges from the core, but were not started themselves; a real phone keyboard — on the emulator the address with a capital was typed by a command, not by the keyboard; the Android card of a new task, where the note is a plain field without links.

**Tables as a grid** (R60) — the core is checked by the tests `r60_table_reports_cells_and_alignment` and `r60_tables_in_a_list_item_and_in_a_quote` in `core/tests/markdown.rs`: cells with Cyrillic and emoji, column alignment, a short and a long row, a table as one block, a table in a list item and in a quote. The web is checked by the test `r60_a_table_comes_with_its_cells` and in headless Chrome through DevTools at window widths 1100 and 380: in a table of three columns and four rows the cells of each column start at the same coordinate and have the same width, the header is built from `th`, the columns have the classes `left`, `center`, `right`, bold, a link and code are kept in the cells, a short row is padded with empty cells, the text before and after the table is in place; a click on the note opens a field with the source text unchanged. Not checked: Safari and Firefox, a table wider than the note. macOS is checked by the debug script in a hidden window, the steps `notes`, `caret`, `type`, `shot`: without the cursor in the table the bars between cells became tabs, the row of dashes and the row edges are hidden, the header is bold, on the snapshot the columns are aligned, the second column centred, the third right-aligned, a cell with emoji does not break the grid; the cursor in a table row shows the whole source text, the cursor outside it shows the grid again; after a character is typed in a cell the note in the database differs from the original only by it; a table wider than the note and a table in a quote stayed source text. Android is checked on a windowless API 36 emulator: on the screenshot the columns of the same table are aligned with the same alignment; a tap on a table row shows the source text, a tap on a line outside it shows the grid (by the field text from `uiautomator dump`). Not checked: resizing the window on macOS with a note open, rotating the screen and a large system font on Android, a table wider than the note and a table in a quote on Android, selecting with the mouse across the grid.

**Icons** — one drawing for macOS, Android (an adaptive icon with a monochrome layer) and the web (SVG, PNG 32, 180, 192 and 512); they are built by the script `scripts/make-icons.py`. Serving the icons by the web server is checked by the test `icons_are_served_before_login`. In the Android icon the whole drawing lies inside a circle of radius 33 dp out of 108, which no launcher mask cuts, and the monochrome layer is drawn separately: the check mark in it is cut out of the circle, because the themed icon takes only the transparency from the layer. Both are checked by the test `AdaptiveIconTest`, which also fails at the former scale of 1.3. Checked 2026-10-08 on a windowless API 36 emulator by screenshots: under the round mask of the Pixel launcher the drawing is whole and stands with a margin from the edge; the themed icon on the home screen shows the check mark, while with the former layer there was a solid circle in its place. Not checked: masks of another shape (on the emulator the launcher gives only the round one, the tightest), the light theme of the themed icon, a real phone.

**Note mark in the row on Android** (R72) — before the fix the line under the title on Android did not show the note, while on macOS there was an icon. Checked 2026-10-08 on a windowless API 36 emulator (1080×2400, 420 dpi), English interface, dark appearance, `uiautomator dump` and a screenshot, the "Today" view. For three tasks with a note the line under the title has an icon described as "Notes": after the number of attachments and before the tag, and for a task without attachments and tags — between the due date or the repeat sign and the list name; the note text is not in the row. The icon is the height of a line of text (42 px), and the line under the title stayed the same height as for a task without a note. A new task without a note has no icon. The note is erased in the card → after the card is closed the row of this task has no icon. Not checked: the Russian interface and the light appearance, an enlarged system font size, a task that has only a note (a line under the title of one icon), a narrow screen where the line does not fit, a real device. The row on macOS did not change and was not checked again. The Android app has no automated tests for this; the check is manual.

**Look modelled on Things** (R72, R79, R80, #99) — checked 2026-10-08 with builds of the branch. macOS: a hidden instance of the debug build with a separate data folder. View icons: the `viewicons` step of the debug script draws the icons of all seven built-in views to a file in the light and the dark appearance — filled, blue, yellow, pink, turquoise, green and two grey. Large title: the `winshot` step draws the window to a file — in "Inbox" a large "Inbox" with a blue icon stands above the tasks, in a list the list name with its icon in its colour, and there is no title in the toolbar and no line under it. Row: the `rowshot` step for five tasks — a task without fields has nothing after the title; a task with a repeat and an attachment and due today has two signs in "Today" and nothing on the right, and "Today" on the right in "Inbox"; a task due today at 23:30 has "23:30" on the right; a task due yesterday has a red "Yesterday" on the right; all rows are the same height, 258 px at eight pixels per point. Android: a windowless API 36 emulator (1080×2400), English interface, dark appearance, `uiautomator dump` and screenshots. In the panel of lists the seven view icons are filled with the same colours, and the list icons are in the colour of the list. In "Today" an overdue task has the repeat, note and attachment signs after the title and "Yesterday" on the right in the error colour; the tasks for today have the signs and nothing on the right; none has a line under the title. A title of 80 characters takes the row from the mark to the note sign at the right edge (843 of 1080 px), ends with an ellipsis, and the row height is the same, 63 px. The rule for the date at the right edge on Android is checked by the test `RowDateTest`. Not checked: a visible macOS window; the icons in the macOS sidebar itself (a hidden window does not draw its rows, the icons are drawn separately by the same code); where "+", the sync icon and search stand on the screen after the title left the toolbar; macOS 14, where the title is not removed from the toolbar; the note and subtask signs and the parent's title of a subtask on macOS; "Upcoming" on both platforms; the Android light appearance; a real phone. Search as an icon (R80) was checked 2026-10-08 by the same script in a hidden instance, the `search` step prints whether the field is open and its text: before ⌘F the field is closed; after ⌘F it is open and has the keyboard; after typing "milk" one of two tasks remained in the list, the view title is "Search", and in the window drawing the field stands to the right of the title; after Esc the field is closed, the text is empty, and the list has two tasks again. Not checked: a click on the search icon, a visible window, closing the field by leaving it and by switching to another view; after Esc the keyboard stays with the window, not with the list. In the installed build 0.1.0-rc.9 the buttons ended up on the left, right after the sidebar button: without the title nothing pushed them to the right edge. After the fix (a flexible space before the buttons) in the window drawing by the `winshot` step "+" and the search icon stand at the right edge; before the fix the same drawing put them on the left, as on the screen. The visible window after the fix is not checked.

**The note follows the cursor on Android** (R81) — checked 2026-10-08 on a windowless API 36 emulator (1080×2400), English interface, dark appearance, `uiautomator dump` and screenshots; the text was typed through `adb shell input`. Before the fix: the note grows together with the card up to 16 lines, after that the card takes the whole screen and does not scroll — after 28 lines the screen shows lines up to the 22nd, the rest are under the keyboard, and the note field is in the same place (top at 274 px). After the fix: after 30 lines the last line with the cursor stands above the keyboard, the top of the field moved to 64 px, and the title is scrolled past the top of the screen. The keyboard closed, the card opened again, a tap on the 27th line, which would be under the keyboard, and typing "XX": the line with the cursor is visible above the keyboard, with one more line below it. Not checked: typing on the on-screen keyboard with its suggestions, a real phone, a note with markup (headings, lists, a table), where hidden markup changes the height of lines; rotating the screen. The "text goes up" from the complaint was not reproduced in that form: on the emulator the typed text went under the keyboard.

**The card of a new task on macOS** (R37, R82) — checked 2026-10-08 in a hidden instance of the debug build with a separate data folder and its own clipboard (`LISTS_DEBUG_PASTEBOARD`). The `quicknote` step draws the quick entry window: with 228 characters of text from the clipboard — a block labelled "From the clipboard", three lines with an ellipsis and a button, a row of six icons on the right, a bar with the list "Inbox" and the buttons "Cancel" and "Save" (disabled); without text — a note with a placeholder, window height 150 points. The `winshot` step in the main window: the card from ⌘N without the bar, the list as a chip on the left, icons on the right; after the due date «сегодня» ("today") and high priority there are three chips on the left and four icons on the right. Tab from the title: note, due date, priority, list, start date, repeat, "#", attachment, title. Keys: the title "Cancelled one" and Esc — no card, the list is empty; "Saved by return" and Return — the task is created; "Saved by cmd return" and ⌘Return — the task is created. Not checked: a visible window; the bar buttons by click; ⌘Return from the note (the press went with the keyboard in the title); expanding the block by click and from the keyboard; a chip moving to the other side after the picker closes, and that an open picker does not close when a value is chosen; the menu bar window; Esc in the note of the card of a new task; wrapping of the row when there are many chips.

**A lighter look for the card of a new task** (R82, #136) — the decisions of the author of the project of 2026-10-09 are recorded in #136: a regular weight for the title and light icons in all three places, rounder corners of the quick entry window, the placeholder "Notes". Checked 2026-10-09 in a hidden instance of the debug build with a separate data folder, Russian interface, light appearance. The `quicknote` step drew the quick entry window to a file: the title in regular weight at text size, the placeholder «Заметки» ("Notes"), empty space under it down to the row of icons, six light thin icons on the right, a bar with the list «Входящие» ("Inbox") and two capsules — a grey «Отмена» ("Cancel") and a pale (disabled) «Сохранить» ("Save"); the corners are rounded, the height of the empty window is 184 points instead of the former 150. **A lighter look for the card of an open task** (R94, #159) — the decisions of the author of the project of 2026-10-09 are recorded in #159; he asked to tone down and shrink the completion mark by the same comparison with Things. **Android look modelled on Things** (R95, R96, R64, #161) — the decisions of the author of the project of 2026-10-09 are recorded in #161; the model was screenshots of Things 3 for iPhone from the App Store and from the help pages of culturedcode.com; Things has no Android app. Checked 2026-10-09 on a windowless API 36 emulator (1080×2400) on demo data (`cargo run --example demo`), English interface, `uiautomator dump` and screenshots. "Today", dark appearance: in the bar the menu on the left, search on the right, no title; under the bar a large "Today" with a yellow star; "Overdue" and "Today" are set in the text colour with a line; the marks are grey for the tasks of the blue and the green list; "+" is round and blue. The list "Work", light appearance: the list icon in blue before the title. The panel of lists: the rows have no settings icons, and a long press on "Work" opened the list settings. Swiping a row: to the right — a green backing with a check mark, to the left — a yellow one with a calendar; the row was released in place, and the task did not change. The card of the task "Quarterly report": the title in regular weight, on the left the chips "Today, 17:00" (red, overdue), "High", "#reports", below them on the right the icons for start date, repeat, reminder, tag and file; lines between the three subtasks, round marks, a completed one grey with a check mark and without strikethrough. "Stand-up" has the due date, the repeat "Every week" and the tag on the left, the chips moved to a second line, and the icons stand on the third. The repeat icon opens the repeat picker. The card of a new task: after "Call the bank tomorrow 10:00 !!" the chips "Tomorrow, 10:00" and "Medium" on the left, the icons for start date, repeat and file on the right, the list and a blue "Save" in the bar; pressing "Save" created a task due tomorrow at 10:00 with medium priority (a query to the app's database), the card stayed open and empty, "Save" is disabled. The quick entry window from "Share" shows the same card. The screenshots `docs/screenshots/android-*.png` were taken again with the same build. Not checked: choosing a value in the opened picker and the icon turning into a chip (the press in the script missed the item); the expanded card of a new task; the sync icon next to search (sync is not set up on the emulator); an empty view and the large title above it; the titles of a tag, a project and a filter; the Russian interface; TalkBack; a real phone. Not done: the drag handles on subtasks in the mockup — Android has no reordering of subtasks; the title in the card of an open task stands 16 dp to the right of the note, as before.

**The quick entry window does not bring back a hidden app on macOS** (R98, #168) — found by the author of the project 2026-10-09 in the installed build 0.1.0: after "Hide Lists" the global shortcut showed the quick entry window together with the main window; when another app was active and Lists was not hidden, only the quick entry window appeared. Cause: a hidden app is shown whole as soon as one of its windows is brought forward, and the quick entry window is a window of the app; the frameless window style (R82) has nothing to do with it. Reproduced 2026-10-09 in a hidden instance of the debug build with a separate data folder and transparent windows, the steps `ghost`, `hideapp`, `quickopen`, `quickclose`, `unhideapp`, the state is printed by the `apphidden` step. Before the fix: after `hideapp` the app is hidden and there are no main windows in the window list; after the quick entry window is shown the app is shown and the main window is in the list; after closing the app stays shown. After the fix: with the quick entry window open the app is shown, and there are no main windows in the list; after closing the app is hidden again; after `unhideapp` the main window is in the list. The first attempt — setting aside the windows that became visible right after the app was shown — did not work: at that moment they are not yet counted as visible; the windows are remembered at the moment of hiding. Acceptance: on 2026-10-09 the author of the project put the Release build of the branch into `/Applications` and went through, in a visible window with the real global shortcut, the steps he had been given — hide Lists, call up the quick entry window, close it, bring the app back from the Dock, watch whether the main window flashes — and reported that everything was fine. Not checked: switching to Lists with the quick entry window open (named to the author as optional, he did not report on it separately); an app hidden before the first press of the shortcut after a launch with "Hide" in the login item; the settings window open together with the main one; full-screen mode and several desktops.

**The menu bar icon window does not bring back a hidden app on macOS** (R98, R24, #292) — found by the author of the project 2026-10-16: the menu bar icon window, opened while Lists was hidden, brought the main window back with it. The cause is the same as in #168: a hidden app is shown whole, and only the quick entry window knew how to set windows aside. Reproduced 2026-10-16 in the debug build with a separate data folder and transparent windows: the steps `veil`, `hideapp`, `menubaropen` (a real click on the icon) and `apphidden` — after the click the app is shown and the main window is in the window list. The order of events, recorded at the same time: the icon window becomes key and its view appears before the app receives `willUnhide`. The windows are set aside in two steps: in `willUnhide` they become transparent, after `didUnhide` they leave the screen. The first fix (PR #295) removed them from the screen already in `willUnhide`, and after that the app stayed hidden for good: `isHidden` was reset neither when the icon window closed nor on `unhideapp`, and the main window brought out on closing stood on the screen of a hidden app. It was checked by a step that did the same without the icon window and showed the app itself, so it did not see the defect; the step is removed, and `apphidden` now also prints the number of the app's windows that the window server keeps on the screen. While the icon window is open the app is active, and bringing windows back on activation is turned off for that time. The view of the icon window is recreated on each change of the counters (`id`), so opening and closing are marked by the background outside it. Checked 2026-10-16 with real clicks on the icon (the steps `menubaropen`, `menubarclose`; the author of the project did not touch the keyboard during the run): with the icon window open the hidden app is shown, there are no main windows on the screen, after closing the app is hidden again and there are no windows on the screen, the same on the second opening; after `unhideapp` the main window is on the screen; an app that is not hidden does not lose its window; the `menubarlists` step (what "Open Lists" does) with the icon window open brings back the main window and closes the icon window, and the next click on the icon opens it again; that the icon window stayed open after "Open Lists" was noticed by the author of the project during a manual check on 2026-10-16, and now it is closed by a click on the icon's button — the same way the system closes it; the quick entry window over a hidden app still appears alone. Acceptance: on 2026-10-16 the author of the project was given steps in the debug build with an empty data folder — ⌘H, the icon window, a click in another app, the Dock, "Open Lists", "Settings…", entering a task, the Dock with the icon window open; he reported that everything works, but after "Open Lists" the icon window stays open. After the fix the author of the project went through "Open Lists" and "Settings…" with the mouse in the same place and reported that everything was fine.

**Bringing back a closed card of a new task and expanding by drag on Android** (R97, #165) — the decisions of the author of the project of 2026-10-09 are recorded in #165: he chose not to keep a draft, closing erases what was entered, and the undo bar stays for 3 seconds. Before the fix: type "Draft text", close the card with the "Back" button, press "+" — the card is empty and there is nothing to bring the text back with. Checked 2026-10-09 on a windowless API 36 emulator (1080×2400) on demo data, English interface, dark appearance, `uiautomator dump` and screenshots, gestures sent through `adb shell input`. After a card with text is closed, a bar "Draft discarded" with "Restore" stands at the bottom; 5 seconds later it is gone, and "+" opens an empty card. "Restore" opened the card with the title "Call bank tomorrow !!" and the chips "Tomorrow" and "Medium". "+" pressed half a second after closing opened an empty card, and the bar did not appear again. After an empty card is closed there is no bar. Dragging the handle up: the card is expanded (the title line has the "Collapse" button), the text is in place. Dragging the handle down: the card is compact (the "Expand" button) and open; one more — the card is closed, the bar is shown. The first build put the handle of the expanded card under the status bar, and dragging down from it had no effect; after the fix the handle stands lower. The quick entry window from "Share": after text is typed and `user_rotation` is changed twice, the text in the title is the same. The unit tests `NewTaskEntryTest`: what counts as entered, and that everything entered comes back from the saved state. Not checked: bringing back a card with chosen files, dates from the picker and a repeat on the screen (in the script the title and what was parsed from it came back); whether the quick entry window actually rotated — `uiautomator dump` does not show it; a tap outside the card as a way to close it; the undo bar at the same time as the bar for undoing a deletion; TalkBack, for which 3 seconds may be too short; a real phone and dragging with a finger.

**The card of an open task is stretched by the handle, there is no expand button on Android** (R99, R97, R64, #174) — the decisions of the author of the project of 2026-10-10 are recorded in #174: an open task changes only its height, and the expand button on the card of a new task is removed so that the size changes the same way everywhere. Before the fix: open a task and pull the handle up — the card stays as high as its content; this was so on the author's phone, on the emulator it was not captured before the fix, and in the code the card had only the sheet handle, which closes it. The card of a new task meanwhile expanded by the handle in the debug build and in the released 0.1.0 on API 36 and in the debug build on API 28. Checked 2026-10-10 on a windowless API 36 emulator (1080×2400), English interface, `uiautomator dump`, gestures sent through `adb shell input swipe`. The open task "Issue105": the handle at 1705 px; drag up — the handle at 186 px, under the status bar, the title and the "⋮" menu at the top; drag down — the handle again at 1705 px, the card is open; one more — no card. Stretch, close with the "Back" button, open "Rot50Title" — the handle at 1705 px. The card of a new task: there is no "Expand" button; with "Draft" typed, drag up — the handle at 186 px, the send button in the title line, the text in place; down — compact with "Save", the text in place; down once more — the card is closed. The quick entry window from "Share" with the text "Shared174": a handle above the card; up — the card full screen with the send button; down — compact with "Save"; down once more — no window. The decision on the gesture offset is covered by the test `HandleDragTest`. The screenshot `docs/screenshots/android-quick-entry.png` was taken again. Not checked: a real phone and dragging with a finger; the handle action in TalkBack; the "Draft discarded" bar after closing by drag (scenario R97, the closing code did not change); the quick entry window in landscape orientation, where there must be no handle; an open task with a long note and the keyboard open in a stretched card; the light appearance; API below 36.

**Text size on Android** (R100, #173) — the decisions of the author of the project of 2026-10-10: the same five steps as on macOS, the step is multiplied by the system font size, the typeface is not chosen. Checked 2026-10-10 on a windowless API 36 emulator (1080×2400), English interface, the heights of the text lines taken from `uiautomator dump`. The first build set the scale in the Compose theme: the text changed on the main screen and in the quick entry window, but stayed as before in the settings dialog and in the task card — the dialog and the bottom sheet are drawn in their own window and take the scale from the context. After the fix the scale is set in the configuration of the activity, and choosing a step recreates it. "Default": a settings row 53 px, a task title in the list 63 px, the title in the card of an open task 147 px. Right after choosing "Largest", without a restart: the settings stayed open, a settings row 83 px; a task title in the list 91 px, in the card 175 px. After stopping and starting the app the title in the list is 91 px. The quick entry window from "Share": the placeholder "Notes" 91 px against 63. A system font size of 1.3 at the step "Largest": a settings row 96 px, after returning the system size to 1.0 — 83 px. After choosing "Default" a settings row is 53 px, the title in the list 63 px, and 1.0 is written in the app settings. The value from the saved state is covered by the test `TextScaleTest`. The large view title changes little (95 and 99 px): since version 14 Android enlarges large text less than small text. Not checked: the look on the screen by eye and on a real phone; the steps "Small", "Large" and "Larger" one by one; clipping and wrapping of labels at the large steps (in the settings at "Largest" the label "Appearance" wrapped to a second line); the Russian interface; text typed in the settings and not saved when the step changes; API below 36.

**Settings by section** (R101, #178) — the decisions of the author of the project of 2026-10-10: both platforms, five sections, on Android a list of sections instead of tabs. macOS, checked 2026-10-10 in a hidden instance of the debug build with a separate data folder: the `settingsshot` step of the debug script draws the settings to a file from a window that is not shown, the `settingstab` step chooses a tab. On "General" — appearance, text, the sidebar and completed tasks; on "Tasks" — new tasks, quick entry and calendar events; on "Sync" — the storage and "Save and sync"; each tab has its own content height (651, 638, 342, 186 and 287 points). Android, checked at the same time on a windowless API 36 emulator (1080×2400), English interface, `uiautomator dump`: the settings open full screen as the list "General", "Tasks", "Notifications", "Sync", "Backups" and "Import…", with "Settings" and "Close" in the header; "Notifications" → an arrow and "Notifications" in the header, only the notification settings on the screen; "Back" → the list; in "General" appearance, text size and how long completed tasks are kept; in "Tasks" new tasks and calendar events; "Save and sync" is only in "Sync"; in "Sync" WebDAV chosen and an address typed, the arrow to the list and "Sync" again → the address is in place; "Backups" opens the former backups dialog, "Back" from it returns to the list; "Back" in the list closes the settings. The screenshot `docs/screenshots/android-settings.png` was taken again. The real settings window was opened in a visible instance of the same build with ⌘, from the script: a window of 520×450 points on "General", "Sync" and "Tasks" — the window height does not depend on the tab, and content taller than the window scrolls. Not checked: the look of the tab bar on the screen by eye and remembering the tab between launches (the `settings` step of the script does not open the window on this version of macOS, and the hidden app does not show it); keeping what was typed on the "Sync" tab on macOS when switching between tabs (follows from the shared view holding the state, not captured by a run); Android: the Russian interface, the light appearance, "Import…" from the list, the large text steps, a real phone.

**App failures in the log** (R102, #252) — 2026-10-14. Before the fix the apps wrote to the log only the launch (Android) and the launch, login at system start and a refusal of notifications (macOS); the other failures from the list of R102 were swallowed by `try?` and `runCatching`. The reason in the line is the text of the core error or the code of the system error: `localizedDescription` of macOS file errors and `message` of `IOException` and `ActivityNotFoundException` name the file. Covered by the tests `AppLogTest` (4): the reason from a core error, a file error without the file name, one ERROR line on switching to inexact alarms and INFO on switching back. macOS, checked in a hidden instance of the debug build with a separate data folder: the copy for Quick Look was not created (a file lay in place of its folder) → `ERROR attachments: a file was not copied to be shown: NSCocoaErrorDomain 516`. Android, checked on a windowless API 28 emulator: a mark from the widget for a task that does not exist → `ERROR widget: a task was not completed: not found`; a file passed to quick entry at an address that does not exist → `ERROR attachments: a picked or shared file was not copied: FileNotFoundException`, without the file name. Not checked: a refusal of the keychain and Keystore, the system refusing a notification on macOS, a refusal of the UnifiedPush distributor, the permissions for notifications and exact alarms (they do not exist on API 28, and the API 36 emulator was busy), a refusal of the share extension on macOS, opening and sending a file without a suitable app, a failure to read the settings; real devices. A failure to open the database is not written to the log: the core writes the log through the open database.
**Attachment failures in the log** (R102, #251) — 2026-10-12. Before the fix the second pass (S34) silenced the error of each file (`upload_blob(...).unwrap_or(false)`, `download_blob(...).unwrap_or(false)`) and counted as successful, and its result was written only at the "Detailed" level; so the 409 refusal of an upload from #250 left not a single line in the log at the "Errors" level. Reproduced by a test before the fix: zero ERROR lines. Along the way the core started writing failures that the apps discarded without a trace (`try?` in macOS, `runCatching` in Android): download on request, adding an attachment, the notification plan, the push address, the automatic backup and deleting old backups, reading the addresses of other devices. Covered by the tests `core/tests/log.rs` (+8). Not checked: a real WebDAV server (the storage error in the tests is substituted in the `WebDavRemote` format), the lines about the notification plan and the automatic backup before the archive is written (a database failure cannot be caused by a test), the apps on devices; the tests and clippy were run on Rust 1.97, not on 1.94.1 from `rust-toolchain.toml`. Failures that remain only in the app code (Keychain and Keystore, the system refusing a notification, UnifiedPush, the widget, opening and sending files) are not included here.

**Work log** (R102, #177) — decisions of the author of the project from 2026-10-10: a file in the data folder, four levels with "Errors" by default, sharing as a file, the web server is not affected. Before the change, neither the core nor the apps wrote anything; only the web server wrote five messages to the error stream. The core is covered by the tests `core/tests/log.rs` (14): the default level and the absence of the file, an ERROR line when the storage is unavailable, written once when the same failure repeats and again after a successful pass, an INFO line about a successful pass only from the "Events" level, a pass without changes only at "Detailed", the log turned off, the level after reopening, the level does not go to another device, one line per message, rotation of the file after 1 MB, clearing, no title, note or password in the log after a pass with and without a failure, no log in the backup. macOS, checked 2026-10-10 in a hidden instance of the debug build with a separate data folder: on the "General" tab, the "Log" section with the level "Errors"; the "Show in Finder" and "Clear" buttons are unavailable while there is no file (step `settingsshot`); after a start at the "Errors" level there is no file; the "Events" level is written to the database of this folder, and after the next start `log.txt` holds the line `INFO app: started 0.1.0 on macOS …`. Android, checked at the same time on an API 36 emulator without a window, English interface, `uiautomator dump` and the contents of the app folder: after a start at the default level there is no file; in "General", the "Log" section with the row "Level — Errors", no rows for sharing and clearing; after "Events" is chosen, the file holds `INFO log: level set to info`, and "Share the log" and "Clear the log" appeared; after a restart, the line `INFO app: started 0.1.0 on Android 16`; "Share the log" opened the system chooser "Sharing 1 file, log.txt", a copy is in `cache/logs`; "Clear the log" — no file, no rows for sharing and clearing. Not checked: choosing the level and pressing the buttons on macOS in a real window, "Show in Finder"; receiving the file in another app on Android; the lines about a sync failure, backup and import in the apps themselves (the core writes them, covered by its tests); a failure of the push server — the line about it is not covered by a test; the Russian interface of Android; a real phone.

**Keyboard in the card on ⌘N after quick entry** (R37, #152) — found by the author of the project on 2026-10-09 in a visible window of the debug build: after work with the quick entry window, ⌘N in the main window opened a card in which nothing could be typed. The state was taken with the `state` step every two seconds while the author repeated the scenario by hand: the main window is key, the card is open, but the keyboard focus is on the root view of the window (`AppKitWindowHostingView`), not on the title field; before ⌘N it was on the task list. The card's request for keyboard focus on appearing is lost. Why is not established: by a script in a visible window (panel, Esc, panel, a task through it, ⌘N) and in a hidden instance (keyboard focus on the list, a row selected, ⌘N, six times) the failure did not reproduce; a pass through the panel by hand is needed. Refuted by measurement: the main window stays key after the panel closes. The change: the card in the main window, 50, 150, 350 and 750 ms after appearing, checks whether the keyboard focus is on the text, a chip or an open picker, and if not, requests it for the title again. Checked with the same manual scenario on the build with the change: the failure happened three times, each time the card requested keyboard focus again and got it, the title was typed. In a hidden instance the ordinary path did not change: ⌘N gives keyboard focus to the title without a second request, Tab leads to the note and to the chips. Not checked: the "+" button, the menu bar window, the release build; a connection with the flash from #83.

**Documentation and screenshots** (#137) — 2026-10-09. `docs/usage.md`, `README.md`, `docs/architecture.md`, `docs/self-hosting.md` and `docs/releasing.md` were compared with the specification and the code; what differed was fixed. The image `ghcr.io/juev/lists` has no `latest` tag while all releases are pre-releases (checked by a query to the registry), so the installation commands name the version. All screenshots in `docs/screenshots` were taken again from the same demo data, created by the example `cargo run --example demo` in `core/`. Android: an API 36 emulator without a window, dark theme, English interface, `adb exec-out screencap`, scaled down to 720 pixels wide. Web: headless Chromium (playwright-cli) on a server with the demo data, windows 1180×760, 1180×620 and 390×844; the English variant with the browser language substituted. macOS: the main window was captured with `screencapture` in a visible instance started by the author of the project; the quick entry window was drawn by the `quicknote` step without being shown on screen, so it has no shadow. The `winshot` step did not fit the main window: the sidebar in it is empty. The "Today" screenshot on macOS was taken before the change #155, the screenshot of the task card after it. Not compared: the labels of the macOS interface named in `docs/usage.md`, by eye on screen; `docs/specs/sync.md`, `caldav.md` and `import.md` with each other.

**Cut-off card of an open task on macOS** (#155) — found 2026-10-09 while the window was captured for #137: a task card with a note of several lines was cut off at the bottom; the row of fields, the attachments and the subtasks were not visible. The build `v0.1.0-rc.10` behaved the same way. The observations were made in a hidden transparent instance (`ghost`) with the `winshot` step and matched a screenshot of the visible window through `screencapture`. There were two causes. The first: the height of the note is requested before the view has received the new text, and was computed from the previous, empty one; now it is computed from the text passed to the view, and the card receives the note and the attachments of the task when it is created, not after it appears. The second: the first row of the task list does not change its height when its content grows — a file attached to the open card of the first task of a list stayed cut off, while the row of the third task of the same list grew, in whatever order the cards were opened. Why the first row behaves like this is not established; its place is now taken by an empty row one point high. It is absent where the first item is the block of calendar events or a group heading ("Today", "Upcoming"): in the visible window the empty row before a group heading gave an extra gap under the name of the view, and the first task under a heading grows without it — a file attached to the open card of the first task in "Today" is visible. Checked in the same place: a card with a note of four lines, with an empty line, with checkboxes, with an attachment and subtasks is shown in full in the first row and in the others; a file attached to the open card of the first task is visible; a closed list moved by one point, the spacing between rows is the same; the down and up arrows select the first and the second tasks; text typed in the note of an open card stays in it and is written to the database (the height is not computed from the passed text while the note is being typed). In the visible window a card with a note, an attachment and subtasks in the first row of the list is shown in full (the `screencapture` screenshot for #137). Not checked: dragging tasks to the first place, views with group headings ("Upcoming"), typing a long note in the card of the first task.

**Typed text survives the app stopping** (R83, #127) — checked 2026-10-08 with builds before and after the change. macOS: a hidden instance of the debug build with a separate data folder; the text was typed by the debug scenario with keyboard focus in the note, the quit was done by the `quit` step, which calls the same as ⌘Q; the note was read by the `notes` step after a new start. Before the change, after quitting the note is empty, after `kill -9` as well. After the change, after quitting the note holds the typed text; a title extended before quitting is saved. Android: an API 36 emulator without a window, the text was typed through `adb shell input` at the end of the note of an existing task, the note was read from the card after `am start`. Before the change: "Home" and `am kill` in the background — the typed text is gone; `am force-stop` with the keyboard open — the typed text is gone. After the change: "Home" and `am kill` — the typed text is in the note; the same with the task title. Not checked: quitting with a real ⌘Q and from the menu; losing activation on macOS (a hidden app is never active); the app being unloaded by the system itself rather than by a command; logging out and shutting down; the title on Android before the change; a real phone. A force stop, a crash and `kill -9` still lose the text: the requirement does not cover them.

**The "Overdue" group in "Today"** (R84) — before the change, a task without a due date with a start date in the past also landed in "Overdue": the grouping on macOS and Android took the start date when there was no due date, while the overdue counter in the core counted only the due date, so the group heading and the colour of the counter disagreed. Checked 2026-10-08. macOS: a hidden instance of the debug build with a separate data folder, a task "Started" with the start yesterday and a task "Late" due yesterday, the window drawn by the `winshot` step. Before the change both are under "Overdue"; after it, "Late" is under "Overdue" with a red "Yesterday", and "Started" is under "Today". Android: the rule was moved to `dueDayPassed` and checked by the test `DueDayPassedTest`. Not checked: the Android screen (the emulator was not started), the visible macOS window, the colour of the counter next to the group.

**"Won't do" outside the card** (R69) — checked 2026-10-08. macOS: a hidden instance of the debug build with a separate data folder, the card of a task open; Tab from the title goes: note, start, due, priority, "+", title — there is no "Won't do" chip in the row; the `wont` step, which calls the same as the context menu item, moves the task to "won't do". Android: the item was removed from the "⋮" menu of the card, the build and the tests pass. Not checked: the context menu of the row on macOS itself and the long-press menu on Android (their code did not change), the Android screen, the web.

**Attachment type from the content** (R85, I8) — found on real data: two tasks moved over from 2Do carried an attachment "Image" with the type `application/octet-stream`; the file is intact, it is a JPEG 2160×4320. In the 2Do backup it has the name "Image" and the extension "jpg" in a separate column, and the import took the extension only when there was no name. Checked 2026-10-08: the core with the tests `r85_*` in `core/tests/store.rs` and `i8_*` in `core/tests/import.rs`; on a copy of the real database (the database itself was opened read-only) the example `lists … files` gives both attachments as "Image.jpg". macOS: a hidden instance of the debug build with a separate data folder, a file "Image" with the same content attached to a task; the `files` step prints the name "Image.jpg", the type `public.jpeg`, a thumbnail of 28×56 and one request to show it in Quick Look; in the database the name stays "Image" and the type `application/octet-stream`. Not checked: the visible window and the Quick Look display itself, saving the file from the app, Android and the web (both take the name and the type from the same call of the core), a repeated import from 2Do over tasks already imported.

**Saving an attachment** (R86) — checked 2026-10-08. macOS: a hidden instance of the debug build with a separate data folder, an attachment "Image" with JPEG content; the `savefile` step calls the same function as "Save As…" after a location is chosen: a new file is written, a file over an existing one is replaced, both match the original by SHA-256 (563 779 bytes); writing to a folder that does not exist returns an error. Android: an API 36 emulator without a window, English interface, `uiautomator dump`: a file in the card has a "⋮" button with three items; "Save to…" opens the system location picker with the name "reg-pic.png", after "SAVE" the file is in `/sdcard/Download`; "Share" opens the system sheet "Sharing 1 file". In the installed build a right click on an attachment opened the task menu, not the attachment menu: the row menu was attached to the whole card and covered the file menu, so its items, the earlier ones included, were unreachable from the screen; this was not checked in the hidden window. The first change was a "⋯" button on the file and moving the task menu to its row: in the visible window the "⋯" items work, but a right click on the file still opened the task menu and dragging did not start. Inside a list row, SwiftUI gives the context menu and dragging to the row, whichever nested view asks for them. The second change: the task menu is back on the whole row, and over the icon and the file name there is an AppKit layer that itself takes the click, the drag and the right click. In a hidden instance the `filepointer` step prints its size 97×28, the menu items "Quick Look", "Open in Default App", "Save As…" and the file "Image.jpg" that a drag will carry; a click on the layer opens the preview. After the second change, in the visible window of the installed build 0.1.0-rc.9, the owner of the project checked on a real attachment: a right click on the icon and the file name opens the file menu, dragging carries the file out of the app, a click on the name opens the preview. Not checked: the save dialog itself on macOS and the error message; dragging from the card to Finder and back; on Android, that the content of the saved file matches the original, the message "Saved", the buttons in the image preview, a real phone.

**Saving all attachments** (R91, #138) — checked 2026-10-09. Core: the tests `r91_*` in `core/tests/store.rs` (three files with the same content; a taken name and two attachments with one name; a name with path separators stays inside the folder; a file without content and a folder that does not exist are named in the result) and `r91_content_that_has_not_arrived_is_fetched_before_everything_is_saved` in `core/tests/sync.rs` (two of three are downloaded before writing; a file that is not in the storage is named, the others are written). macOS: a hidden instance of the debug build with a separate data folder, a task with the attachments "a.txt", "b.pdf", "c.png" and a second "a.txt", the folder already held "a.txt". The `filepointer` step showed a fourth item "Save All…" in the file menu; the `saveall` step calls the same function as the menu item after a folder is chosen: "a 2.txt", "b.pdf", "c.png" and "a 3.txt" were written with the content of the attachments, the earlier "a.txt" did not change; for a folder that does not exist, the result names all four files. Android: an API 36 emulator without a window, English interface, `uiautomator dump`, a task with "a.txt", "b.pdf" and "c.txt". The items "Save all to…" and "Share all" are in the "⋮" menu of a file and in the "⋮" menu of the task. "Save all to…" opens the system folder picker; the system does not allow choosing the root of "Download", so the folder "Download/out" was chosen, which held "a.txt": after "USE THIS FOLDER" and "ALLOW" it holds "a (1).txt", "b.pdf" and "c.txt" with the content of the attachments, the earlier "a.txt" did not change — the free name was given by the documents provider, the app renames nothing. "Share all" opens the system sheet "Sharing 3 files"; passed to "Lists" itself, the files became attachments of a new task named "a.txt", "b.pdf", "c.txt". Not checked on macOS: the system folder picker, the item in the task menu on a right click, an error message on screen, the "downloading…" label on pending attachments while saving is in progress. Not checked on Android: the messages "Saved" and "Could not save: …" (they are not visible in `uiautomator dump`), downloading pending attachments before saving and sending (sync is not set up on the emulator), a documents provider other than the built-in one, the Russian interface, a real phone.

**File name in the share sheet on Android** (R86, R91, #147) — found 2026-10-09 while checking #138: the system share sheet showed a hash of the content instead of the name of the attachment, for one file and for several; the receiving app got the right names. The cause, from `adb logcat`: the sheet (`com.android.intentresolver`) was denied access to the app's file provider ("Permission Denial: opening provider androidx.core.content.FileProvider … that is not exported"), so it could not ask for the name and showed the last segment of the URI, that is, the file name on disk. The sheet takes the read permission from the `ClipData` of the intent, and the URIs were not there. After the change the URIs are there as well. Checked on an API 36 emulator without a window, English interface, `uiautomator dump`, a task with "reg-pic.png" and "reg-shape.svg": before the change "Share" showed "cabd550b…7498", after it "Sharing 1 file" and "reg-shape.svg"; "Share all" — "Sharing 2 files", "reg-pic.png" and "+ 1 more file"; there are no denials in the log; two files passed to "Lists" arrived in the card of a new task. There is no unit test: the behaviour belongs to the system sheet and can only be checked on a device. Not checked: "Share" from the image and PDF preview (it calls the same function), other Android versions, a real phone.

**Title on one line** (R73) — before the change, the title in a list row on both platforms took up to two lines. Checked 2026-10-08 with builds before and after the change. macOS: a hidden instance of the debug build with a separate data folder, the `rowshot` step of the debug scenario draws a row 320 points wide, text size 1.15. The row of a task "a" is 210 px high; the row of a task with a title of 113 characters was 338 px before the change and 210 px after it, and the title in the screenshot ends with an ellipsis. The `edit` step opens the card of this task, the `state` step prints the text of the title field: the full title. Android: an API 36 emulator (1080×2400, 420 dpi) without a window, English interface, dark theme, `uiautomator dump` and a screenshot, the "Today" view. A title of 109 characters took 126 px in height before the change, 168 px together with the line under it, against 105 px for the neighbouring task; after the change the title is 63 px, 105 px together with the line under it, and the title in the screenshot ends with an ellipsis. A tap on the row opens the card, the title field holds the full title. Not checked: the visible macOS window (the row is drawn outside a window by the same code); a narrow and a wide window; the other text sizes and typefaces of macOS; an enlarged system font on Android; the Russian interface and the light theme of Android; a completed task with a struck-through title, a project and a task with a priority; a real phone. The web interface did not change and was not checked: the requirement does not mention it. The title field in the card (#112) was checked 2026-10-08 by the same means. macOS: a hidden instance of the debug build with a separate data folder, the `edit` and `state` steps; the `state` step now also prints the size of the field that receives typing. With a title of 113 characters the field was 512×17 points before the change and 512×36 after it, with the full title in it. Typing and Return: the title is saved with the typed text, keyboard focus is on the list, there is no line break in the title; ⌥Return and typing — no line break either. Android: an API 36 emulator (1080×2400) without a window, `uiautomator dump` and a screenshot. The field with a title of 8 characters is 147 px high; after typing a title of 133 characters it is 336 px, in the screenshot the title takes four lines and is visible in full, the keyboard shows a "Done" key. Enter from the keyboard (`KEYCODE_ENTER`): the text of the field did not change, there is no line break; after the card is closed, the list row is 63 px with the same title. Pasting text of several lines on Android is checked by the test `OneLineTest`. Not checked: the visible macOS window and the height of the table row under the grown field; pasting text of several lines on macOS; pressing the "Done" key of the on-screen keyboard; a title longer than the screen; a real phone. The card of a new task did not change on either platform: its title field is still one line.

**Time in the date dialog on Android** (R74) — before the change, "Add time…" was only in the first choice of the dialog and only for a date already set: a day and a time took two openings. The conversion of a day from the calendar and the text of the "Time" row are covered by the tests `MomentTest` (`r74_*`). The dialog was checked 2026-10-08 on an API 36 emulator (1080×2400, 420 dpi) without a window, English interface, dark theme; the screen was read through `uiautomator dump` and a screenshot, the dates from the app database. The card of a new task, start date: "Pick a date…" → under the calendar a row "Time" with the value "Off" → 20 October → "Time" → 18:30 → "Done" → the dialog is closed, the icon shows "tue, 20 oct, 18:30"; "Pick a date…" again → the row shows "18:30" → 21 October → "Done" → the database holds `2026-10-21T18:30`. The card of a task, due date: 22 October → "Done" → a due date without a time; 23 October → "Time" → 07:15 → "Done" → a due date with a time in one opening; 24 October → "Done" → the database holds `2026-10-24T07:15`. "Tomorrow" for the start date closes the dialog with one tap. In the reminder dialog there is no "Time" row; after "Done" in the calendar the time picker opens. Landscape orientation: the row is under the calendar and does not cover it, but the calendar is left with one full week; rotating with the time picker open keeps it open, "Done" saves the day and the time. Not checked: scrolling the calendar to the other weeks in landscape orientation (a gesture through `adb` did not move the weeks, the cause was not found); the Russian interface and the light theme; an enlarged system font; a screen narrower than 360 dp; the keyboard date entry mode of the calendar; TalkBack; a real phone. There is no automated test of the dialog itself; it is checked by hand.

**Portrait orientation on Android** (R77) — before the change, reproduced on an API 36 emulator (1080×2400, 420 dpi) without a window: after `settings put system user_rotation 1` the main window takes 2400×1080. After the change, checked 2026-10-08 in the same place through `dumpsys window`: the main window stays 1080×2400 when rotated either way and when the app is started on a rotated device. The quick entry window from "Share" over the system settings in landscape orientation opens in landscape, and a typed title survives rotation to portrait and back; over the main Lists window it is portrait. A wide display (`wm size 1600x2560`, `wm density 240`, shorter side 1067 dp): the main window rotates on API 36, where the system removes the lock, and on API 28, where the app removes it; on the same API 28 emulator with a phone size the window does not rotate. In the same place, without restarting the app, the display was returned from wide to a phone size, as with a folded foldable phone: the window is portrait again and does not rotate. Which windows are locked in the manifest is held by the test `OrientationTest` (`r77_*`). Not checked: split screen, a foldable phone and a real tablet, rotation of a real device by its sensor, the quick entry window from the tile and the shortcut, Android 8.0.

**Calendar events in "Today"** (R78) — events are read by the shell, not the core: EventKit on macOS (`apple/App/CalendarEvents.swift`), `CalendarContract.Instances` on Android (`CalendarEvents.kt`); they do not reach the database or sync. The order, the time and which day an event belongs to on Android are covered by the tests `CalendarEventsTest` (`r78_*`): the system stores an all-day event from midnight to midnight in UTC, so its day is read in UTC, not in the time zone of the device — otherwise, west of UTC, "Today" would get tomorrow's event, and east of it yesterday's. Android was checked 2026-10-08 on an API 36 emulator (1080×2400, 420 dpi) without a window, English interface, dark theme; the screen was read through `uiautomator dump` and a screenshot. Two local calendars and events were created through `content insert`. The setting is off → no block, `READ_CALENDAR` not granted. The switch → the system dialog → "Don’t allow" → the row "Lists has no access to the calendar". The switch again → "Allow" → under it both calendars with checkmarks → in "Today" above the tasks "Birthday", "09:00 Standup", "14:30 Bank"; yesterday's and tomorrow's all-day events are absent. The checkmark of a calendar removed → its events are gone, its number is written to the settings of the device. An event added while "Today" is on screen → the row appeared without any action in the app. In "Upcoming" there is no block. A tap on an event → the system started `EventInfoActivity` of Google Calendar; the event page itself was not seen: the emulator's calendar does not know local calendars and closed with "No calendars found". A day without tasks on the device was not checked. macOS was checked in the same place by a hidden start with a separate data folder: the `calendarevents` step showed that the setting is off and access has not been requested from the system; the step `events:14:30=Bank,-=Birthday,09:00=Standup,y=Trip,09:00=Alpha` gave the rows "Birthday", "Trip", "09:00 Alpha", "09:00 Standup", "14:30 Bank", the block is shown in "Today" and not shown in "Inbox". Not checked on macOS, because it needs a visible system dialog and the person's calendars: the access request, reading real events, the list of calendars in the settings, how the block looks on screen, a click on an event, the effect of the entitlement `com.apple.security.personal-information.calendars` in a signed build with hardened runtime.

**Backups** (R87–R90, S40) — the core is checked by the tests `core/tests/backup.rs`: a backup is restored in a clean installation with the same lists, tasks, tags, attachment, trash and setting; the settings of the device are not included in the backup; the schedule and rotation; a file that is not a backup, a backup of another format and a damaged one do not change the database; attachment content that does not match its name and a path outside the folder are not taken; restoring with a merge and with a replacement of the storage data between two devices; a backup restored on another device does not share an identifier with the first one. Android was checked 2026-10-09 on an API 36 emulator without a window, English interface, sync off; the screen was taken from `uiautomator dump`, the data from the app database, the files from the app folder: on the first start of the build an automatic backup appeared; "Create backup now" added a second one, the list shows the date and the size; after a task was added, "Restore…" on the second backup and confirmation — the task is gone, the number of live tasks is the same as before, the device identifier changed, the list has a third backup with the state before the restore, and no temporary files are left in the backup folder. Not checked on Android: "Save a copy…" and "Restore from file…" (they need the system file picker), deleting a backup, changing the schedule and the number of backups from the dialog, restoring with sync on, a backup with large attachments, the background job with the app closed, the Russian interface, a real device. The section in the macOS settings is built but has not been run.

**Backup before a database migration** (R92, #144) — the decisions of the author of the project from 2026-10-09 are recorded in #144: make a backup, an ordinary one and in the rotation, by the schema version number. Checked by the tests `r92_*` in `core/tests/backup.rs`: a database with a lower number gets a backup before the migration, the backup is restored in a clean installation with the same tasks, the next start creates no backup; a clean installation, a database without a number, a database without records and a database with a higher number get no backup; the backup is created even when the database has no derived tables at all; when the backup cannot be created, the database does not open and its number stays the same; with five backups, the new one pushes out the oldest. The schema version number is currently 1, and there have been no migrations that raise it yet: "a database of the previous schema" in the tests is a database with the number set to 0. Not checked: a real migration that changes the table of fields (there is none), starting the macOS and Android apps and the web server on such a database — they open the database with the same call of the core, and what each of them shows when the database did not open because of the backup was not looked at.

**Menu bar icon and hiding from the Dock** (R116, #224) — the decisions of the author of the project from 2026-10-12 are recorded in #224: a switch for the menu bar icon, hiding from the Dock only while the icon is shown, the task count is not shown without the Dock, a choice about the window at login, the names of the settings. Found out before the code: the Dock icon is removed and returned on the fly by changing the activation policy of the app (`NSApplication.setActivationPolicy`), no restart is needed; the menu bar icon is a `MenuBarExtra` with an `isInserted` binding.
