Checked 2026-10-12 in a hidden instance of the debug build with a separate data folder with the `dock` step of the debug scenario: "Hide Lists from the Dock" → policy `accessory`, back → `regular`; turning the icon off with the Dock hidden → policy `regular`, hiding turned off, the menu bar icon window hidden; asking for hiding with the icon off → not accepted; after a restart the settings are the same and the policy is applied at start; the app keeps its main menu (eight items), so the shortcuts have something to respond to. A start taken as a start at login (`LISTS_DEBUG_AT_LOGIN`) with "Show the window at login" off → the decision "stay hidden" is taken. Not checked, because it needs a visible screen or a real login: that the Dock icon and Lists in ⌘Tab really disappear; that the shortcuts of the main menu work in the app window without the Dock; that "Open Lists" and "Settings…" bring the windows to the front; that the system marks a start at login the way the app recognises it (an open event with the login item flag), and that the window does not appear on such a start — the hidden instance is hidden without that anyway; how the settings look on screen.

**One keychain prompt after an update on macOS** (#241) — noticed by the author of the project on 2026-10-12: after an update the keychain asked for the password twice. The cause, from the code: at start two items were read, the storage password and the push server token, and a build with an ad hoc signature is a different app for the keychain after an update. The change: the secrets are kept in one item, and the earlier ones are moved over on the first read. Checked 2026-10-12 in a hidden instance of the debug build with a separate data folder with the `keychain` step of the debug scenario, on test items (`org.evsyukov.lists.debug`), not those of the installed copy: two secrets stored the old way opened two items on the first read and moved over; on the next read "from scratch" both secrets were obtained from one opened item; a deleted secret does not come back from the earlier item, and the earlier item is deleted; a saved secret is read from the shared item. According to `security find-generic-password`, the shared item exists, the items of the installed copy are untouched, and the test items were deleted after the check. Not checked: the keychain prompt itself on screen — how many times it is asked after a real update will be shown by the next update of the installed copy; the first start of the version with this change will ask twice, once for each earlier secret.

**Start does not wait for the helper restart** (R115, #247) — found 2026-10-12 while checking 0.3.3-rc.1 installed on the Mac of the author of the project: a start that came a few seconds after the previous one took 9–10 seconds instead of one. The cause was established by measurement on the same Mac: `launchctl kickstart -k` stops the helper at once and waits until the system starts it again, and the system keeps ten seconds between two starts of the same agent — the second command in a row took 10.0 s against 0.0 s for the first; Lists waited for the command on the start thread. In 0.3.2 and earlier the same wait was at the end of the start, with the window already shown. The change: the command runs off the start thread. Checked 2026-10-12 with a hidden instance of the debug build with a separate data folder and a debug agent of the helper, the time from start to exit by the `quit` step: before the change, a start right after the previous one — 12.1 and 10.2 s, after 11 seconds — 2.0 s; after the change — 2.1, 2.0 and 2.1 s. The helper works again after the start: at once when the previous one was started long ago, and after the system pause following two starts in a row (stopped at 18:38:49, started at 18:38:55). The installed helper was not restarted meanwhile; `scripts/check-launch.sh` passes. Not checked: the installed build with this change.

**Helper restart at the start of Lists** (R115, #238) — found 2026-10-12 while updating the installed Lists to 0.3.1: 15 seconds after the new version started, the helper was still a process of the previous build. The cause was established from the crash reports: that start (process 97274) began at 17:09:20 and stopped at 17:11:09 without reaching the end — first it waited for an answer to the keychain prompt, then it crashed (#239), and the helper was restarted only at the end of the start. The restart itself worked: 0.3.2 restarted the helper as soon as the start reached the end, after 36 seconds of waiting for the keychain. The change: the helper is restarted first thing. Checked 2026-10-12 in a hidden instance of the debug build with a separate data folder and a debug agent of the helper (`org.evsyukov.lists.helper.debug`), which does not touch the installed helper: the helper was turned on by the step `loginitems:helper=on`; Lists was started with a start delay of 10 seconds (`LISTS_DEBUG_HOLD_START=10`) — the previous helper was stopped in the very first second, the new one started in the eighth, while the start was still delayed; eight seconds is the system's own pause between starts of the same agent, the previous one had been started three seconds before. The installed helper was not restarted meanwhile. Not checked: an update of the installed copy with a real keychain prompt.

**Count on the Dock icon** (R103, #175) — the decisions of the author of the project from 2026-10-10 are recorded in #175: the options "Today", "Overdue tasks" and "Do not show", "Today" by default, the "Notifications" section, macOS only; Android moved to #182. The core computes the counts (`Store::counts`), the shell sets the icon badge (`NSDockTile.badgeLabel`). Checked 2026-10-10 in a hidden instance of the debug build with a separate data folder; the `dockcount` step of the debug scenario prints the choice and the badge: a new installation — choice "Today", no badge; tasks due yesterday and today — "2"; "Overdue tasks" — "1"; the overdue one completed — no badge; "Do not show" — no badge; "Today" — "1"; after a restart the choice is the same; the app hidden, a task "c today" from the quick entry window — "2". Not checked: the count on the icon by eye in the Dock, the choice in the settings window on screen (the step sets the choice through the same model property as the settings), recounting when the day changes, a change that came from the Share extension and from sync.

**Persistent notification with today's tasks on Android** (R104, #172) — the decisions of the author of the project from 2026-10-10 are recorded in #172: the titles of the "Today" tasks and a new task button, a swiped-away notification comes back at once, it comes back after a reboot, off by default, its own low-importance channel. The selection of titles is checked by the tests `r104_*` in `TodayNoticeTest`. Checked 2026-10-10 on an API 36 emulator without a window, English interface, through `uiautomator dump`, `dumpsys notification` and the system event log. A clean installation with the tasks "a" (due yesterday) and "b" (due today): the switch "Today's tasks in the notification shade" is off, there is no notification. Turn it on → a notification in the channel `quick_add` with importance 2 and the ongoing flag: "Today: 2", the rows "a" and "b", the button "New task". The button in the expanded notification in the shade opens `QuickAddActivity`; a task "c today" from it → "Today: 3", the rows "a", "b", "c". Mark "a" completed in the app → "Today: 2", the rows "b" and "c". Swipe the notification away in the shade → the event log shows a cancel by the person's gesture and a new post 5 ms later, the notification is in the shade. Seven tasks → five titles and "2 more". Device reboot, the app not opened → the notification is in place with the same rows. The day change alarm is set to 00:00:01 of the next day. Turn it off → no notification, the alarm is cancelled. A clean installation without the notification permission: turning it on shows the system request, there is no notification before the answer, after "Allow" — "Nothing for today" with the button. Not checked: how the notification looks by eye, the Russian strings on screen, updating the content at midnight (the alarm is set, its firing was not observed), a change that came from sync, Android older than 14, the lock screen. After installing a build over the previous one the notification is in place; whether it was removed and returned by the receiver in the process is not established. Since R106 the notification channel has changed its identifier to `today_notice`: the earlier channel `quick_add` gave a badge on the launcher icon, and the app cannot change that for an existing channel, so the channel is deleted.

**Count on the app icon on Android** (R106, #182) — the decisions of the author of the project from 2026-10-10 are recorded in #182: the count is carried by its own silent notification in its own channel; a launcher that can only show a dot shows a dot and the count in the long-press menu; the options are the same as in R103, "Do not show" by default. That the notification is persistent and comes back after being swiped away, as in R104, was not decided in #182 and was chosen during implementation: otherwise "Clear all" would remove the count until the next change of tasks. Before the code, from the Android documentation ("Notification badges") and the Launcher3 source (`NotificationListener.notificationIsValidForUI`), it was established that the badge on the launcher icon comes only from an active notification, and that a persistent notification not from the default channel gives it. The choice of the count is checked by the tests `r106_*` in `IconCountTest`. Checked 2026-10-10 on an API 36 emulator without a window (image `google_apis`, Pixel Launcher), English interface, through `uiautomator dump`, `dumpsys notification` and the system event log; the count on the launcher icon was read from the icon's description in the launcher's app list ("Lists has N notifications"), and an overdue task was obtained by moving the emulator clock a day forward. A clean installation with the tasks "a" (due yesterday) and "b" (due today): "Count on the icon" — "Do not show", no notifications. "Today" → a notification in the channel `icon_count` with importance 2 and the ongoing flag: "Today: 2", the count 2; the launcher icon has "Lists has 2 notifications". "Overdue tasks" → "Overdue: 1", the launcher icon has "1 notification". Mark "a" completed → no notification, no count on the launcher icon. "Today" → "Today: 1". "Do not show" → no notification and no count. The R104 notification turned on, "Do not show" → a notification of the channel `today_notice` (the channel badge is off) in the shade, no count on the launcher icon; together with "Today" → the launcher icon has "1 notification", not two. Swipe the notification away in the shade → the event log shows a cancel and a new post 6 ms later, the notification is in place. Reboot, the app not opened → "Today: 1" is in place. Day change, the app not opened: the clock set to 23:59:45, nothing to count; the notification "Today: 1" appeared between 00:01:31 and 00:01:41 — the day change alarm is inexact, and the system may delay it by up to an hour. Without the notification permission, choosing "Today" shows the system request, there is no notification before the answer, after "Allow" — "Today: 1". A reminder in the shade next to "Today: 2" → the launcher icon has "Lists has 3 notifications". Not checked: the badge on the launcher icon by eye (a dot or a number), the long-press menu of the launcher icon, launchers of other manufacturers, the Russian strings on screen, a change that came from sync, Android older than 14, installing over a build with the `quick_add` channel.

**Updating Android notifications when the day changes** (R66, R104, R106, #191) — the decision of the author of the project from 2026-10-10 is recorded in #191: the day change alarm is exact where "Alarms & reminders" is already allowed; the app does not ask for a new permission; without the permission the alarm stays inexact. There is no unit test: the behaviour is entirely in `AlarmManager`. Checked 2026-10-10 on an API 36 emulator without a window through `dumpsys alarm` and `dumpsys notification`, with the R104 notification and the "Today" count turned on, one task due tomorrow. Without the permission: both alarms are set to 00:00:01 of the next day with a window of an hour. The permission granted (`appops set … SCHEDULE_EXACT_ALARM allow`), the app not touched → both alarms with a window of 0. The clock set to 23:59:50: at 00:00:00 the shade shows "Nothing for today" and no count, at 00:00:02 — "Today: 1" in both notifications; the alarms are set to the next midnight, also exact. The permission revoked → the system stopped the app, there are no alarms; after the app is started they are set with a window of an hour. Not checked: a device left asleep overnight (the alarm does not wake the device and fires when it wakes), Android older than 12, how late the inexact alarm actually is.

**List settings on Android: long press and the "Configure" item** (R95, R105, #187) — the author of the project reported on 2026-10-10: on a phone with the v0.1.0 release, a long press on a list in the panel does nothing; the settings do not open and the list is not selected. The author's decision from 2026-10-10: add a visible path — a "Configure" item in the menu of the open list (R105); the long press stays. The long-press failure was not reproduced. Checked 2026-10-10 on an API 36 emulator (1080×2400, 420 dpi, gesture navigation) without a window, gestures through `adb`. A debug build from `main` on the demo data (`cargo run --example demo`): a still press of 1 s on "Work" opened the list settings in the English and in the Russian interface; a press drifting evenly over a second by 10 and 30 px horizontally and by 30 px vertically did too. The v0.1.0 release (the APK from the release page): a press of 1 s on "Inbox" opened its settings; no list was created in the release build. A press in which the finger moves 25 px right, left, up or down right after the touch and then stays still for a second does not open the settings and does not select the list; with a 12 px move the settings open. The system threshold is 8 dp, 21 px on this screen: beyond it the gesture is taken by the panel itself (horizontally) or by its scrolling (vertically). This matches the author's description, but it is not confirmed as the cause of the failure on the phone: the phone model, the Android version and the system long-press delay were not recorded. Later the same day the author reported (#188): on the phone the failure no longer reproduces; a long press opens the settings in the panel of lists and the task menu in a list; what changed between the attempts is not established, the long-press code did not change. The choice of what "Configure" opens is checked by the tests `r105_*` in `ConfiguredTest`. A build of the branch on the same emulator, English interface: in "Work", "Inbox" and the filter "Next 7 days" there is "More" after search, and "Configure" in its menu opened the settings of the list, of "Inbox" and of the filter; in "Today" and in the tag "#home" there is no button; with search open in "Work" the bar holds only the button that closes search; a long press on "Work" in the panel still opens the settings. Not checked: a real phone; a release build of the branch; the sync icon before "More" (sync is not set up on the emulator); the Russian interface of the menu; TalkBack.

**Widgets with tasks and the new task button in them on Android** (R107, R108, #170, #171) — the decisions of the author of the project from 2026-10-10 are recorded in #170 and #171: the view is chosen on placement ("Today", "Inbox" or a list), a task can be completed with its mark, a tap on the heading and on a task opens the view, there are two sizes (2×2 and 4×4), there is no separate widget for a new task — the "+" sits in the widget with tasks, and the task goes to the default list of quick entry. The widget draws the rows itself and does not scroll: what does not fit is counted by the row "N more". The choice of view, the number of rows by height, the row "N more" and the order of "Today" are checked by the tests `r107_*` in `TaskWidgetTest`. Checked 2026-10-10 on an API 36 emulator (1080×2400, Pixel launcher) without a window on demo data (`cargo run --example demo`), English interface, `uiautomator dump` and screenshots. The widget list has two widgets of Lists: "Tasks, small" (2 by 2) and "Tasks, large" (4 by 4) with a description. Small: on adding, a choice of "Today", "Inbox", "Work", "Home", "Trip to Kazan" opened; after "Today" the widget has the heading "Today", "+" and three tasks in the order of "Today", the overdue one first. The mark of the first task: the row left, and in the app database the task has a completion time. With the app open in "Inbox", a tap on the title of a task in the widget brings the app to "Today". "+" opened the quick entry window. Large, list "Work": the heading "Work" and the open tasks of the list; a tap on the heading opens the app in the list "Work" — both when the app is open in another view and when its process is killed (`am kill`); there stays one instance of the screen. A task completed in the app left the widget; the mark in the large widget removed one more. After a new build was installed over the old one, the widgets were drawn again. Appearance: dark and light following the system (`cmd uimode night`). Found and fixed during the check: a load started before the view was chosen returned the previous view — a just created screen opened in "Today" rather than in the list of the widget. Not checked: the row "N more" and "No tasks" on the screen; resizing the widget and the number of rows after it; changing the choice through the widget settings; refresh after sync, after a reboot and on a change of day; a widget of a deleted list; a repeating task completed from the widget; the list a task from "+" goes to with another default list; the Russian interface; Android below 12; other launchers; a real phone; TalkBack. After the app is force-stopped (`am force-stop`), the widgets are empty until the app is started again. The check on the emulator ran before the branch was moved onto `main` with the badge counter (R106); after the move the build and tests passed, and the emulator check was not repeated.

**Selecting several tasks in the web interface** (R118, #231) — the decisions of the author of the project from 2026-10-12 are recorded in #231: a selection mode as on Android, an action bar at the bottom, one undo snackbar. Chosen during implementation and not decided in #231: the mode is entered with a "Select" button in the header, because the web interface has no row menu; due date and start date are set as a date without a time; a tag is typed in a browser window. What an action does to each task and how it is undone is computed by the server, and this is checked by the test `r118_*` in `web/tests/api.rs`: priority, skipping a task that already has it and restoring the previous one to each; a tag, recorded as in the core; due date; moving and moving back; completion, skipping a closed task; Trash, where only restoring applies, and its undo; refusing to take another's operation as an undo step. The page was checked 2026-10-12 in a headless browser over the debugging protocol, on a local server with a separate data folder, Russian interface: "Select" turns on the mode with "Selected: 0" and unavailable actions; two rows are selected by taps, the card did not open; priority from "More" changed both and showed "Changed: 2", "Undo" restored the previous ones (one had low); "Delete" showed "Deleted: 2"; in "Trash" the selected tasks have one button "Restore", after it the trash is empty and "Restored: 2", "Undo" returned both to the trash. Not checked: use with a finger on a phone, picking a date in the browser's system calendar, the tag entry window, the dark appearance, records of completed repeats on the page.

**Selecting several tasks on macOS** (R117, #227) — the decisions of the author of the project from 2026-10-12 are recorded in #227: four ways to select, the action set of Android, a context menu and the existing keys and menu bar items, Return does nothing, one undo, any row can be selected. Chosen during implementation and not decided in #227: Esc leaves one row selected; "Due" and "Start" have no "Choose…" (for one task this item opens the card); for a subtask a group action only changes its state and deletes; tasks in the trash and records of repeats get their own items when only they are selected. The core did not change: an action is the same calls as for one task, one per task, and undo is one step with the reverse calls. Checked 2026-10-12 in a hidden transparent instance of the debug build with a separate data folder, with the steps `pick`, `pickmore`, `pickto`, `extend`, `selectall`, `unpick`, `picked`, `batch`, `batchmenu`, `undo`, `task` of the debug script; the selection steps call the same code as clicks with keys. A click with ⌘ added and removed a row; a click with Shift selected a range of four rows, and `extend` and real presses of ⇧↓ changed it; ⌘A selected all ten rows of "Today", Esc left one; Return with several selected did not open the card; Space with three selected completed three tasks, undo reopened them. Priority: of five selected, five changed, undo restored the previous one to each (one had "Low"). Due date on another day: three tasks left "Today" and the selection, undo brought them back. The tag «#Дом» was recorded as «дом» ("#Home" folded to "home"), moving to another list and undo: a task of a project returned to its project. "Won't do" and undo; deleting two tasks; in "Trash" the menu of the selected tasks is one item "Restore", "Complete" changes nothing there, restoring brought both back. Not checked: real clicks with ⌘ and Shift on rows, ⌘A from the keyboard and the keys ⌫, ⌘T, ⇧⌘T, ⌘Return with several selected — they need visible work with the window; the context menu on the screen and the "New tag…" window; records of completed repeats. Noticed during the check: two actions in a row, given as steps of the debug script, are undone with one undo, and two single "Complete" actions behave the same in the build before this change. Found 2026-10-12 (#230): this is a property of the script, not of the app. The step `undostate` showed that after a press that the script passes to the app directly, the change group in the undo manager stays open even two seconds later, the same in a hidden and in a visible active window. When the same two "Complete" actions are given as real presses through the event queue (`scripts/postkey.swift`) to a visible window, the group is closed, the first undo reopens only the second task, the second undo the first.

**Project dates for its tasks** (R125, #264) — the decisions of the author of the project from 2026-10-14 are recorded in #264: a due date taken from the project stands in the row in place of the due date, in a muted colour with the project icon, an overdue one in red with the icon; the card shows only the task's own dates; the start date of a project gives no dates to a task without dates when the project has no due date; a task waiting for the start of the project stands in "Upcoming" on the start day. Chosen during implementation and not decided in #264: the daily summary does not count the project itself, its due date shows there through its tasks; the project's own reminder and the notification for its due date stay as they were. The effective dates are computed by the core in SQL on read (`effective_due`, `effective_start` of `TaskItem`) and are not written to the data; checked by the tests `r125_*` in `core/tests/store.rs` and `web/tests/api.rs`. Checked 2026-10-14 in a hidden instance of the macOS debug build with a separate data folder, with the steps `go`, `rows`, `task`, `rowshot`, `notifications`: for a task without a due date the row shows the project's due date muted with a folder icon, for a task with its own due date the ordinary due date; the reminder is scheduled by the project's due date. Android was built and passed the unit tests; the row was not checked on the emulator. The web page was checked by an API test and by reading the script; it was not opened in a browser.

**Completing a project from the sidebar** (R126, #274) — the decisions of the author of the project from 2026-10-14: the project row is a round mark filled by the share "done/total"; open tasks are closed together with the project by R11. Chosen during implementation and not decided in #274: a repeating task of the project is closed for good; in the web interface a button above the tasks of the project replaces the panel menu; on Android the panel closes after completion so that the snackbar with undo is at hand. The core did not need changes: the tests `r126_*` in `core/tests/store.rs` and `web/tests/api.rs`. Checked 2026-10-14. macOS: a hidden instance of the debug build with a separate data folder, the steps `projects`, `completeproject`, `undo`, `rows` — the project leaves the sidebar, "Inbox" opens, one undo brings back the project and the tasks closed with it, a task completed earlier stays completed; setup and check ran in two launches, because the steps of one launch fall into one undo group (#230). Android: an API 36 emulator without a window — the mark is drawn as a circle with a filled third, the long-press menu of a project with tasks has the single item "Complete project", a tap on the mark closes the panel and shows the snackbar, "Undo" brings back the project. Web: a browser without a window — the mark filled by 120° at 1/3, a button above the tasks of the project, a tap on the mark switches to "Inbox" with a snackbar, "Undo" brings back the project. Not checked: the mark on the macOS screen by eye (a hidden window draws no rows) and a mouse click on it.

**Creating a project** (R127, #279) — the decisions of the author of the project from 2026-10-15 are recorded in #279: a dialog with only a title, the list is taken without asking; at once on macOS, Android and the web. Chosen during implementation and not decided in #279: the default values of the list for new tasks (R2) are not given to a project, because the due date of a project becomes the due date of its tasks (R125); in the view of a project a new project goes to the list of the open project rather than becoming its task; on Android the drawer closes after creation; there is no undo of creation, as for a new list. Core: `Store::create_project` — one record with the "project" flag, the tests `r127_*` in `core/tests/store.rs` and `web/tests/api.rs`, the choice of list on Android — `NewProjectListTest`. Checked 2026-10-15. macOS: a hidden instance of the debug build with a separate data folder — the section "Projects" with no projects is visible in the accessibility tree; the step `newproject` (the same call as the "Create" button of the dialog) in the list Work with a default priority and "due today", in the view of a project and in "Today" created projects in Work, Work and "Inbox", without a due date and priority, and each time the view of the new project opened; an empty title created no project; the item "New Project…" in the "File" menu is available. Android: an API 36 emulator without a window — "Projects" and "New project" in the panel with no projects, a dialog with the field "Title", "Create" does nothing with an empty title, after typing "Trip" the view "Trip" opened, and the panel has the project with a mark. Web: a headless browser — the section "Projects" and "+ New project" with no projects, after a title is typed the project "Vacation 0/0" is in the panel and its view is open. Not checked on the screen: the dialog and the "+" button in the section heading on macOS — a hidden window does not draw the rows of the sidebar, and the "+ New list" menu was not opened.

**The list, cards and sidebar of macOS on AppKit** (R37, R82, R114, R117, R130, #307, #308) — the decisions of the author of the project from 2026-10-16 are recorded in the plan of #308: the task list is a flat `NSTableView` with its own rows, the card of an open task and the card of a new task are AppKit views, the same card of a new task sits in the quick entry window and in the menu bar window; the sidebar is its own table, and the selection in it does not fade when the keyboard is with the tasks (as in Things 3). The app has no event interceptors (`addLocalMonitorForEvents`). A new task goes first or right under the selected one (R130). Checked by the debug script in a hidden instance with a separate data folder: the heights of rows by R122 (26/30/40, in "Today" 41/45/55) and of the sidebar (32 and 19 points) are the same as in the former SwiftUI list; selection, keys, menus of rows and subtasks, the card with popovers and Esc in two steps (R62), files, subtasks, tasks of a project, drag data and reordering, a drop on the sidebar, the card of a new task under the selected one, the quick entry window (Esc discards, Return and ⌘Return save, height 176 points). The author checked on a visible window: the flicker of #307 is gone both when a card opens and when a task is created; dragging onto the sidebar and reordering with the mouse work. Not checked: the menu bar window, the shadow of the card and the height animation by eye, ⌘V with files in the card.

**Undo of any action on a task** (R131, R11, R68, R109, R117, R118, R128, #311) — the decisions of the author of the project from 2026-10-17 are recorded in #311: the history is in the core, the stack in memory, on Android a snackbar after each action and an "Undo" item in the top bar menu, in the web interface Ctrl/Cmd+Z outside input fields, each login session has its own history, a task changed after the action is skipped, and the other tasks of the action are restored. The core is checked by the tests `core/tests/undo.rs` (`r131_*`) and `r131_undo_leaves_a_task_changed_on_another_device_and_reaches_it` in `core/tests/sync.rs`, the web by `r131_each_session_takes_back_its_own_actions` and the earlier tests of batch undo, moved to `undo`. The web client was checked 2026-10-17 in headless Chromium: the snackbar "Created"/"Task completed" with "Undo" with the R68 setting "5 seconds", Ctrl+Z on the page undoes, in an input field it does not. macOS: the hidden debug script — ⌘Z (`undo`) removes the due date and priority, then moves the created task to the trash; the steps of the script fall into one `UndoManager` group (#230), so separate ⌘Z presses from the keyboard are not checked. Android: built, the unit tests `SelectionTest` are green, not run on a device. Not decided in #311 and chosen during implementation: there is no snackbar after editing the title and the note, the history depth is 100 steps.

**Swiping past a threshold on Android** (R132, R65, R131, #310) — the decisions of the author of the project from 2026-10-17: the icon appears as the finger moves, at the threshold it turns bright with a vibration, the action happens only on release past the threshold, after completion there is an "Undo" snackbar (R131). The threshold of 40 % of the row width was chosen during implementation. The threshold rule is checked by the test `SwipeTest`. 2026-10-17 on an emulator without a window (`input swipe`, row width 1080 px): a slow swipe right by 350 px and a flick by 300 px in 60 ms did not complete the task; a swipe by 600 px completed it, showed "Task completed" with "Undo" and gave one vibration (`performHapticFeedback(constant=23)` in `dumpsys vibrator_manager`); the "Undo" item in the menu brought the task back after the snackbar left; a swipe left by 300 px opened nothing, by 600 px it opened the due date picker. How it feels in the hand is not checked.

**Reopening a record of a repeat from "Completed"** (R133, R128, R131, #312) — the decisions of the author of the project from 2026-10-17 are recorded in #312: the mark of a record is enabled only while the completion can be undone by R128; a record whose completion cannot be undone carries a repeat icon instead of the mark, and it does nothing; reopening goes into the undo history. The "can be undone" flag is computed by the core on reading a task (`TaskItem.undoable`), and the clients show the repeat icon by it in any view, not only in the log. The core is checked by the tests `r133_*` in `core/tests/store.rs` and `r131_r133_reopening_a_record_from_completed_is_taken_back` in `core/tests/undo.rs`, the web API by `r128_the_completion_of_a_repeat_is_taken_back` in `web/tests/api.rs`, the Android mark rule by `MarkStateTest`. The web client was checked 2026-10-17 in headless Chromium: in "Completed" a record that can be undone has the ✓ button, a record after a due date change has the ↻ icon; a tap on ↻ changed nothing, a tap on ✓ returned the previous due date to the task and removed the record, Ctrl+Z brought the record back. macOS is built, not checked on the screen; Android is built, not run on a device.

**The browser's date in the web interface** (R135, #315) — #315 proposed two ways: the browser's date with each request, or `TZ` and a zone database in the image. The first is chosen: it works for several zones on one server and when travelling, and the image does not change. The core got `Store::in_zone`: for the duration of a call, "now" is read at the given offset, the same way `Store::as_actor` sets the undo history. Checked by the tests `r135_today_is_the_day_of_the_zone_the_call_is_made_in` (core: "Today", overdue tasks, quick entry, the moment of completion and the end of the day for two zones with a fixed clock) and `r135_each_browser_gets_today_of_its_own_zone` (HTTP, two zones against one server). The web test fails if the server does not read the header. The page was not checked by hand in a browser.

**Login to the web interface without busy threads** (R134, #314) — the decisions of the author of the project from 2026-10-17 are recorded in #314: a limit of 5 failures per minute with a 429 response, the client by `X-Forwarded-For` only with `LISTS_TRUST_PROXY=1`, no more than 1000 unfinished OIDC logins with the oldest evicted, `Secure` by the scheme of `LISTS_WEB_URL`. Before the change a wrong password held one of four worker threads for 800 ms; the test `r134_wrong_passwords_do_not_hold_the_server` failed on the old code: the page `/` answered after 805 ms with four wrong logins. The test `r134_unfinished_bodies_do_not_hold_the_server` fails if requests are again parsed in the shared thread: `tiny_http` reads a body up to 1 KB itself, before it hands over the request, so the test promises 2000 bytes. The test `r134_concurrent_guesses_do_not_pass_the_limit` found during self-review that of 20 simultaneous attempts the password was checked 10 times: the limit was compared before the failure was recorded; now the attempt is recorded under the same lock where the limit is compared. A read timeout did not go into the server. On macOS a timeout on the listening socket is not passed to accepted connections, and Linux passes it, but on Linux the same timeout makes `accept` fail, and `tiny_http` stops accepting connections: checked 2026-10-17 in the container `rust:1-bookworm` — after 12 seconds of idleness the server answered "Connection refused". The login limit, the login body, the address from `X-Forwarded-For`, resetting the count, `Secure` and the OIDC bound are checked by the tests `r134_*` in `web/tests/api.rs` and `web/tests/oidc.rs` on macOS and in the same container on Linux. Not checked: work behind a real Caddy or nginx and their timeouts from [self-hosting.md](../self-hosting.md); load from many clients at once.

**Silent data loss on macOS** (R136–R139, #319) — the decisions of the author of the project from 2026-10-17: deleting a list only asks for confirmation, there is no restoring of a list (R131 does not undo actions on lists); an extension without an app group shows an error in the form rather than writing to the queue; a task that could not be created whole is undone the same way as ⌘Z and goes to the trash. Checked 2026-10-17 with a hidden instance of the debug build with a separate data folder: the card "alpha" with the tags `home` and a tag longer than 196 bytes → `finish` → the message "a tag is longer than 196 bytes", the card is open with "alpha" and both tags, "Inbox" has no rows; the long tag removed → `finish` → one open "alpha" with the tag `home`, and the trash has one "alpha" — the one that was rolled back. The check of the file path in the extension (R137) was run by a separate script: a file in the folder, a file through a symbolic link into it, a path with `..` and a path through `/tmp` instead of `/private/tmp` are recognised as lying in it, a neighbouring file with the same start of the name is not; a hard link cannot be recognised this way. Not checked: the form of the extension in a build without a team and passing a file from the group container (needs the "Share" sheet on the screen), the menu bar window during task completion and sync (the step `menubaropen` takes the keyboard), the question on deleting a list (a modal window), an error message over the quick entry window and the menu bar window. Android deletes a list without asking, as before this change; this is fixed in #330.

**Deleting a list on Android** (R136, #330) — "Delete list" in the list settings asks "Delete the list “Work” and move its tasks to the trash?" with the name of the list. Checked 2026-10-17 on an emulator without a window with demo data, English interface, through `uiautomator dump`: a long press on "Work" → "Delete list" → the question with the name → "Cancel" → the list settings are open; the same with "Back"; "Delete" → no settings, "Inbox" is open, "Work" is not in the panel of lists, the trash has 6 tasks, in the database the list has `deleted = 1`. The first run without the change in `MainViewModel` left the deleted list in the panel until a restart: `select` started its own reload, which read the lists before the deletion and arrived later than the reload after it. Now the view switches without a separate reload; three runs in a row — the list is not in the panel. There is no automated test: the Android app has no Compose tests, and `MainViewModel` works with the real storage. Not checked: the Russian text of the question on the screen.

**Data loss on Android** (R139, R143, R88, S53, #320) — the decisions of the author of the project from 2026-10-17: a field in which nothing was typed takes the incoming edit when focus leaves it, and typed text wins without a conflict message; the card of a new task behaves as on macOS (R139), there is no retry for a file that was not attached; backups are made by a separate background job, the age of the last backup is not shown on the screen. Checked by the tests `TypedEditTest` (R143), `ReadForTest` (results of an old search do not replace new ones) and `r139_a_new_task_that_failed_half_way_is_taken_back_whole_and_saving_again_makes_no_twin` in `core/tests/undo.rs`. Checked 2026-10-17 on an emulator without a window: in the quick entry window "beta #" and a tag of 197 Latin letters → "Save" → the window is open with what was typed, the database has no task "beta"; "Share" with a nonexistent file → the task "File" is created without an attachment, the window is closed, the log has an ERROR line `attachments`. The debug build turns on StrictMode: on a cold start, in the quick entry window, in the settings and in backups only `SharedPreferences` accesses the disk from the main thread, and there are no core calls among the violations. In `dumpsys jobscheduler` the backup job has an interval of about an hour and no network requirement, while the sync job has one. Not checked: an edit from another device with the cursor in the field (R143, needs a second syncing copy), closing the settings while the side is being replaced (S53, needs WebDAV storage), rollback of a partly written task on Android (an error in the middle of a write cannot be caused from the interface; the path is checked by a core test), a backup appearing without a network after a day (R88), the text of Toast messages and the snackbar on the screen.

**Quick start of the web server** (#316) — the `docker run` commands in `README.md` and `docs/self-hosting.md` publish the port only on `127.0.0.1`, set `LISTS_TRUST_PROXY=1` like `compose.yaml`, and name the image 0.5.2 instead of 0.2.0, which does not sync with the storage of the 0.5.1 apps. The example without Docker no longer listens on `0.0.0.0`. `.env` and `.env.*` are in `.gitignore`, the template is `.env.example`. `scripts/check-version.sh`, which CI runs, compares against the build version the image tags in both documents, the Status line in the README and `LISTS_VERSION` in `.env.example`. Checked 2026-10-17: the script passes on the current tree and fails if 0.2.0 is put back in `docs/self-hosting.md` or another version is set in `.env.example`; `git check-ignore` ignores `.env` and `.env.local`, but not `.env.example`; `docker compose config` with a `.env` from the template without a password stops at `LISTS_WEB_PASSWORD`, with a password it gives the image 0.5.2 on `127.0.0.1:8080`; `docker manifest inspect ghcr.io/juev/lists:0.5.2` finds amd64 and arm64; a container started with the command from the README on another port is published only on `127.0.0.1` and answers 200 on `/`. A reverse proxy was not run.

**Projects in the list picker** (R129, #293) — the decisions of the author of the project from 2026-10-16 are recorded in #293 and in the discussion of the issue: projects stand as a separate group "Projects" under the lists; choosing a list for a task of a project is an ordinary move that takes it out of the project; `@name` in the quick entry line does not choose a project; the web interface changes to the same extent. Chosen during implementation and not decided in #293: for one task what is unavailable (a project, a task with subtasks, a task that is already in this project) is shown as a disabled item rather than hidden; the new task field in the web interface had no list picker, and none is added; a task of a project created from the card does not get the default values of the project's list, just like one typed in its view. Core: `Store::move_to_project` puts the task at the end of the project and in its list and refuses a project, a task with subtasks and a subtask; web server — the operation `moveToProject` and the batch action `project` with the skips of R123, in the batch `move` a subtask is now skipped rather than breaking the batch. Tests: `r129_*` in `core/tests/store.rs` and `web/tests/api.rs`, on Android `SelectionTest.r129_*` and keeping the project of the card in `NewTaskEntryTest`. Checked 2026-10-16. macOS: a hidden instance of the debug build with a separate data folder — the step `movemenu` showed in "Move to list" the group "Projects" with "Renovation", disabled for a task with a subtask and for a task of this project; of three selected tasks, one of them with a subtask, two were moved to "Renovation", to the end of the project; undo in a separate run (#230) returned the task to its previous list; the card of a new task with "Renovation" chosen and the subtask "glue" created a task of the project with this subtask (verified by a database query), in the view of a project the card shows the project, and "Home" chosen in it created a top-level task in "Home". Web: a headless browser — "Move to list" has the group "Projects"; of three selected, two were moved, "c" with a subtask was skipped; "Home" for a task of a project took it out of the project, "Undo" returned it to the project first; in the "Move to" field of the card "Renovation" is unavailable for a task with a subtask and for a task of the project. Not checked: Android on an emulator (the "Move to list" dialog, the long-press menu, the cards of a new and an open task, the quick entry window) — checked by the build and unit tests; on macOS the menu and the card were not opened on the screen — a hidden window does not draw rows, and the check went through the same menu descriptions and calls as a click.

**⌘A in the task list on macOS** (R117, #276) — ⌘A from the keyboard did not select rows: in #227 it was checked by the step `selectall`, which calls the selection directly, and a real press was not checked. The cause was found 2026-10-14: the press is taken by the item "Edit → Select All" of the menu bar, which sends `selectAll:` to the first responder — the table that draws the list; the table answers it itself and selects nothing, and the press does not reach `.onCommand`. Now ⌘A is caught before the menu bar by the same key press interceptor as in the date editor, and while the keyboard is not with a text field or the card, it selects all rows of the view. Checked in a hidden transparent instance of the debug build with real presses through `scripts/postkey.swift`: before the change ↓ moved the selection and ⌘A selected nothing; after the change ⌘A selected all three rows of "Inbox", and in the task title field it left the rows alone. Not checked: text selection by ⌘A in a field on the screen and a mouse click on the item "Select All" in the menu while the keyboard is with the list — this path still leads to the table.

**The key that shows the main window on macOS** (R124, #258) — the decisions of the author of the project from 2026-10-14: by default there is no key, it is recorded in the settings; a second press with the main window in front hides Lists; the quick entry helper does not listen to it. Chosen during implementation and not decided in #258: the key stands as a separate group "Show Lists" under "Quick Entry" on the "Tasks" tab; when the settings window or the quick entry window is in front, a press brings the main window forward rather than hiding Lists. A closed window stays in the app's list of windows off screen, and it is opened again by SwiftUI, like "Open Lists" in the menu bar, rather than by bringing the same window forward. Checked 2026-10-14 in a hidden transparent instance of the debug build with a separate data folder with the steps `closemain`, `showlists`, `mainwindow`, `hideapp`, `apphidden`, `sidebar` of the debug script; `showlists` does the same as a press, but without bringing the app forward. After `closemain` the main window is off screen, after `showlists` it is on the screen again, and the sidebar has the same 7 rows; after `hideapp` and `closemain` the app is hidden, after `showlists` it is shown with the main window and the same 7 rows. The answer "hide" comes out only for an active Lists with the main window in front — `mainwindow` prints this. Not checked: a real press of the recorded key, bringing Lists forward, hiding by a second press, restoring a minimised window, a press with the quick entry window or the settings window open — they need visible work with the window and the keyboard.

**Dragging selected tasks on macOS** (R123, #257) — the decisions of the author of the project from 2026-10-14 are recorded in #257: the targets are "Inbox", lists, projects and "Today" (due date today); the pointer carries the title of the row and the number of tasks; Android has no dragging. Chosen during implementation and not decided in #257: "Upcoming" does not accept tasks — without choosing a day it cannot give them a due date; a task with subtasks is not moved into a project, because the core keeps it at the top level (R10); one dragged task goes the same way as several and is also undone with ⌘Z, and a task that already stands in the list it is dropped on is not moved to its end. The core did not change: a move into a project is a call of `moveTask` with the project among the parents and the last task of the project in `after`. Checked 2026-10-14 in a hidden transparent instance of the debug build with a separate data folder with the step `drag:row>target` of the debug script: it takes what the drag of a row carries and drops it on a row of the sidebar with the same call as a drop; the data was set up in a separate run, and each undo was checked in its own run (#230). Three selected tasks were dropped on a list and came back with one undo; with two selected, an unselected one was dragged — only it was moved, and the selection stayed; three tasks went into a project after its task in the order of the view, undo returned them to "Inbox"; of a task with a subtask and a task without one, the second went into the project; a drop on "Today" set the due date 2026-10-14, undo removed it; "Upcoming", a list where the tasks already stand, and a project dropped on itself did not accept the drop. In the regression of 2026-10-16 a row could not be picked up with the mouse at all: the press gestures of a row (R117) took the mouse from the list, and neither dragging nor reordering in a list with manual sorting started (#300). Clicks on a row are now handled by AppKit under the row without taking the mouse; the author checked with the mouse a click, ⌘ and Shift, a double click, dragging one and several tasks onto the sidebar with the selection unchanged, reordering, the mark and a right click. Not checked: the look of the preview at the pointer and the highlight of the sidebar rows under it.

**Selecting tasks in the trash and records of repeats on Android** (R109, #232) — the decisions of the author of the project from 2026-10-12 are recorded in #232: the restriction is lifted, the selected tasks of the trash have one button "Restore", records have "Delete", with a mixed selection there is the ordinary bar and records are skipped. The cause of the former restriction was in the code, not in a requirement: such rows have a menu of one item, and no selection action fitted them. What an action does to a task in the trash and to a record and how it is undone, and which bar is shown, is checked by the tests `r109_*` in `SelectionTest`. Checked 2026-10-12 on an API 36 emulator without a window, English interface, taps through `adb shell input`, the screen state from `uiautomator dump`: two tasks are selected in "Today" and deleted; in "Trash" a long press shows "Select" and "Restore"; after both are selected the bar has one button "Restore"; after it the trash is empty and the snackbar says "Restored: 2"; "Undo" returns both to the trash. Not checked on the emulator: records of completed repeats and a mixed selection in "Completed" — only by tests.

**Selecting several tasks on Android** (R109, #163) — the decisions of the author of the project from 2026-10-10 and the chosen mock-up are recorded in #163: entry by the item "Select" in the task menu, an action bar at the bottom, all actions from the list, undo in one step, an action closes the mode, subtasks are selected as rows, dragging across the circles. Chosen during implementation and not decided in #163: due date and start date can only be set, they cannot be removed from several tasks; on undo of a move the task goes first; after an action that changed nothing there is no snackbar. The core did not change: an action is the same calls as for one task, one per task. What an action does to a task and how it is undone, recounting the selection and dragging are checked by the tests `r109_*` in `SelectionTest`. Checked 2026-10-10 on an API 36 emulator without a window (1080×2400, 420 dpi), English interface; gestures given through `adb shell input`, the screen state taken from `uiautomator dump` (a row in selection mode is a checkable element), data from the app database. Six tasks "a"…"f" in "Inbox". A long press on "b" → "Select" first in the menu; tap → "Selected: 1", "b" selected, no new task button, at the bottom "Complete", "Due", "Move to list", "Delete", "More". Tap "d" → "Selected: 2", once more → "Selected: 1". Dragging across the circles from "a" to "c" in one gesture → "a", "b", "c" selected; without releasing, back to "b" → "a" and "b" selected. "Delete" → the mode is closed, snackbar "Deleted: 2", "Undo" → both are in place. "a" and "c", "Due" → "Tomorrow" → "Changed: 2", both are due tomorrow; the same again → no snackbar. "b" and "d", "Due" → "In a week", "Undo" → no due date. "a" and "c", "Complete" → "Completed: 2", both completed; "b" and "d", "Complete", "Undo" → both open. "Back" in the mode → the mode is closed, the data are the same. "b", "Move to list" → "Work" → "Moved: 1"; in "All" "b" (already in "Work") and "f" are selected, "Move to list" → "Work" → "Moved: 1", "Undo" → "f" is in "Inbox" again, first. "More" → "Priority" → "High" → "Changed: 2"; "Tag" → "home" → "Changed: 2", "Undo" → no tags; "Start" → "Tomorrow", "Undo" → no start date; "Won't do", "Undo" → both open. In the mode a swipe right on a row and a long press change nothing. In "Completed" a completed task has "Select", the "Clear…" bar is replaced by the action bar for the duration of the mode; "Complete" on a completed task → no snackbar; "Delete" of two, "Undo" → both are in place. "Done" closes the mode. With zero selected ("Selected: 0") the bar buttons are unavailable, a tap on "Delete" does nothing. Not checked: the look of the mode by eye (background, circles, bar), the Russian strings on the screen, dragging with a finger on a device and in a long list, selecting a parent task together with its subtask, a repeating task in the selection, a task removed by sync during the mode, rotating the screen in the mode, TalkBack.

**Own date picker card on Android** (R110, R74, #162) — the decisions of the author of the project from 2026-10-10 and the mock-up they were made on are recorded in #162: quick options "Today", "Tomorrow", "In a week"; the time is set in the same card, a reminder is not; the card does not accept a date in words; there is one card for the due date, the start date, a left swipe and a reminder. The initial day and time and the month grid are checked by the tests `r110_*` in `MomentTest`; the tests `r74_*` about translating a day from the system calendar are removed together with it. Checked 2026-10-10 on an API 36 emulator (1080×2400) without a window on demo data (`cargo run --example demo`), `uiautomator dump`, screenshots and queries to the app database. English interface, a left swipe on the row "Water the flowers": the card "Due" with three quick options, "October 2026" between arrows, the week from Sunday, today's date outlined, the selected one filled, a "Time" row with "Off", at the bottom "Remove", "Cancel", "Done". Choose the 20th, "Time", type 18 and 30, "Done" — the database has the due date 2026-10-20T18:30. Due date 2026-10-20T18:03: choose the 22nd, "Done" — 2026-10-22T18:03; open and tap "Done" without changes — the due date is the same; choose the 23rd — 2026-10-23T18:03; "Tomorrow" — tomorrow at 18:03, the card is closed; "Time", "Remove time", "Done" — a due date without a time; the forward arrow showed "November 2026", choose a day and "Cancel" — the due date is the same; "Remove" — no due date. Russian interface, a reminder from the card of a task: the heading "Напомнить" ("Remind me"), "Октябрь 2026" ("October 2026"), the week from Monday, the time fields are expanded and set to 09:00, there is no "Убрать время" ("Remove time") button, "Завтра" ("Tomorrow") did not close the card, "Готово" ("Done") — a reminder tomorrow at 09:00. Found and fixed during the check: the swipe handler of a row held the task as it was when the row was first shown, and the card after a swipe opened with the previous due date, and "Done" without changes put it back. A difference from the mock-up: "Remove" stands next to "Cancel", not at the left edge. Not checked: the start date; the card of a new task and the quick entry window; a short window and landscape orientation; a month of six weeks on the screen; typing the time on the on-screen keyboard with a finger (in the script the digits went as keys through `adb`, and `input text` entered the minutes wrong); the light appearance; a text size larger than normal; TalkBack; a real phone.

**Closing the card on macOS by a click outside it and Esc from the sidebar** (R44, #259) — reproduced by the steps from #259 on 2026-10-14 in a hidden transparent instance of the debug build with a separate data folder: Esc from the title, from the note and after a click inside the card closed it, but a click on an empty place of the list did not close the card, and after a click on the sidebar the keyboard stayed with it and Esc did nothing. A click outside the card is now caught by AppKit before the views under it get it: a click on a row of the list stays with the list (it selects the row and closes the card by R38, and closing earlier would shift the rows under a drag that has started), a click on the scroll bar stays with the scrolling. The step `state` of the debug script now notes that the keyboard is with the sidebar. Checked 2026-10-14 in the same setup, with clicks and presses of the debug script: a double click opened "alpha" with the cursor in the title; a click on an empty place of the list closed it, and "alpha" stayed selected; a click on an empty place of the sidebar closed the card and left the keyboard with the sidebar; the chevron expanded the card, the keyboard stayed with the sidebar, Esc closed the card and returned the keyboard to the list; a click on "beta" with "alpha" open selected "beta" and closed the card; what was typed in the title and in the note before a click outside was saved, and after a click on the view heading the keyboard is with the list. Not checked: real mouse clicks and Esc from the keyboard in a visible window; that a click in a popover of the card (date, repeat, tags) and in a file preview does not close it — a hidden app does not show them; a click on an empty place inside the card of a project outside the open card of its task.

**Attachments without reading them whole** (R140–R142, S52, #318) — found by reading the code 2026-10-17: `add_attachment` read the file into a `Vec<u8>`, the second pass (S34) uploaded and downloaded the content the same way, `/api/file` built the response in memory, there was no size limit anywhere except embedded CalDAV attachments; the data folder was created with the permissions of the process, and macOS added attachments and imported on the main thread. Now the core copies the file into `blobs/` in chunks with SHA-256 computed along the way, checks the limit per file and per task (by default 512 MiB and 2 GiB, on the device, not in the shared settings) and removes the content if the task did not accept it; `Remote` can write and read through a file, `DirRemote` and WebDAV do it as a stream, the fallback path `MOVE` goes through a temporary file; the web server receives an upload to disk and serves an attachment from disk, understanding `Range`; the data folder and `blobs/` are `0700`, the database, log and content are `0600`. Checked: `make test` (all suites, including `r140_*` in `core/tests/store.rs` and `core/tests/backup.rs`, `s52_*` in `core/tests/sync.rs`, `r140_*` and `r141_*` in `web/tests/api.rs`), `make lint`, `make apple`, `make android`; a hidden instance of the debug build with a separate data folder and the step `attach:` to the selected task with a 16 MiB file: `files` showed the attachment `large.bin` with its content in place, 16 777 216 bytes in the data folder, `drwx------` on the folder and `blobs/`, `-rw-------` on the content and the database. Streaming in the core is proven by a fake storage whose byte `get` and `put` fail on content, while the file methods count calls. Not checked: a real WebDAV server (in particular, PUT with `Content-Length` instead of chunked, and behaviour on a dropped connection), a large file through WebDAV, the process memory (not measured; instead, the absence of whole reads and the tests), the visible macOS window and that it does not freeze, the "Share" extension, import on macOS, Android (adding and import already ran in `Dispatchers.IO`; only a `TooLarge` branch for the message is added, not checked on a device), CalDAV (it has its own limits of 5 MB per file and 20 MB per task), simultaneous uploads and real devices.

Deviations from the requirements: none.

**Dragging on Android** (R157, #373) — 2026-10-18. The order and the drop position are checked by the tests `ReorderTest`: where rows swap, where a row lands, by how many rows the list scrolls, a press released in place and one brought back to its place. On an API 36 emulator without a window, on demo data, with the gestures `adb shell input motionevent`, checked: "Work", dragged two rows down, became third, and the database recorded the order "Home, Trip to Kazan, Work". A long press on "Home" without movement opened its settings. In the list "Home" with manual sorting, "Buy groceries" was dragged to the top, and the database recorded it first. A long press on a task without movement opened the menu. A swipe right still completed the task. In the same list with 28 tasks (13 fit on the screen), the first task held at the bottom edge for two seconds scrolled the column to the end and became 28th. Not checked: the look of the lifted row on the screen.

**A custom repeat and its end on Android and in the web interface** (R15, #374) — 2026-10-18. Android: the test `RepeatEndTest` checks the choice of the end (the dialog opens on the end of the rule, only the chosen one remains, the date is a day without a time). Web: the test `r15_a_custom_rule_from_the_page_is_kept_whole` sends the rule in the form the page sends it, with `null` in the empty parts. In headless Chromium through Playwright, on a server with a separate data folder, passed: "Custom…" opens the form under the fields. The rule "every 2 weeks, Mon and Wed, 3 repeats" is saved. The form opened again shows the same rule. Replacing it with "the last Friday every 2 months, from completion, until 2027-03-31" is saved without the previous number of repeats. Not checked: the Android dialog on the screen.

**Web notifications and the page without a network** (R146–R149, #335) — 2026-10-17. The notification window of the core is checked by the test `r144_due_notifications_are_those_of_the_window`. The encryption is compared against the example of RFC 8291, the VAPID signature is verified with the public key (`web/src/webpush.rs`). Sending by minutes, the browser settings, the clock change in the zone `Europe/Berlin`, removal of the subscription after 410 and the test notification are checked by the tests `web/src/notify.rs` with a fake delivery service; the API by the test `r145_r146_notifications_over_the_api`. Checked in headless Chromium through Playwright: the service worker keeps the page; without a network a reload shows "The server cannot be reached.", and "Try again" after the network returns shows the tasks. The form with a substituted subscription saves the settings and the zone on the server. Signing out removes the subscription. Messages delivered to the worker through CDP are shown with the text "Due: today" and "Tasks due today or overdue: 3". A notification with the same key replaces the previous one. The address from a notification opens the task in the open page. Not checked: a real subscription and delivery through the Google, Mozilla and Apple services (headless Chromium does not get a subscription), display on the screen, an iPhone with Lists on the Home Screen.

**API tokens** (R150–R152, #344) — 2026-10-17. Storing the token as a hash, surviving a restart, revocation, the check of the name and the scope and the limit of 600 requests per minute are checked by the tests `web/src/tokens.rs`. The tests `r150_r151_tokens_read_write_and_are_revoked` and `r152_tokens_are_limited_and_wrong_ones_count_as_failed_logins` check over HTTP: reading and a 403 refusal on writing with a "Read" token; a task created with a "Read and write" token without `X-Lists`, and its undo with the same token; 403 on managing tokens, signing out and the subscription; 401 after revocation; 429 with `Retry-After`; wrong tokens, after which a password sign-in from the same address gets 429. Checked in headless Chromium through Playwright: the form creates a token and shows it; `curl` with this token creates a task; "Revoke" removes the token, and `curl` gets 401. The token is in neither the server log nor `api-tokens.json`. Not checked: Shortcuts, Tasker and Raycast.

**Backups in the storage** (R153, R154, S60, S61, #347) — 2026-10-17. The core is checked by the tests `core/tests/storage_backups.rs` on a folder:
- a backup without `blobs/` is uploaded once per period;
- a device keeps its own number of backups and does not touch those of others;
- an upload failure does not break the pass and is recorded in the state;
- the switch, "Never" in the schedule and CalDAV produce no backups;
- in an encrypted storage the backup is sealed, and the unencrypted one is deleted;
- a backup is restored on another device, and the attachment comes from the storage.

The S49 tests now turn backups off for the device that plays the previous version: that version made no backups. macOS and Android are built, SwiftLint, ktlint and the Android unit tests pass. Not checked: the settings section on the screen on both platforms and uploading through a real WebDAV server.

**Export** (R155, R156, I11, #349) — 2026-10-17. The tests `core/tests/import.rs` check three things:
- the full round: export from A, import into an empty B, the exports match without identifiers, a repeated import changes nothing;
- the export of one list and the refusal of a format version newer than its own;
- Markdown line by line.

Web: the test `r155_r156_the_export_downloads_as_a_file`; in headless Chromium the "Export…" form downloaded `Lists-2026-10-17.md` with a task from the Inbox. macOS and Android are built, lint and the Android unit tests pass. Not checked: the "Export" menu on macOS and the settings item on Android on the screen, saving through the system file picker.

- R24, R25: receiving files through "Share" is written, not checked live.
- Sync with a folder is not offered on Android: the app has no access to shared folders.
- The texts of sync errors come from the core and are shown in English in both interface languages.
- Web: no drag and drop (a custom repeat is set there since #374); the server is meant for one user (everyone signed in sees the same data). Sign-in is a password and/or OpenID Connect with a list of allowed addresses; checked by the tests `web/tests/oidc.rs` with a fake provider, not run with a real provider. The signature of the ID token is not verified: the token comes directly from the provider over TLS, which OpenID Connect Core 3.1.3.7 allows.
- iOS: there is no build target, nothing was built or run; a phone without Android is served by the web interface. The quick entry window, the menu bar, the keychain and opening files are written in AppKit and will need replacing.
